# Toyota Hiace (2012) - Repair Procedures: Chassis, Suspension & Brakes

> **Source:** Toyota Workshop Manual | **Section:** Repair Procedures: Chassis, Suspension & Brakes

---

### REAR AXLE SHAFT > INSTALLATION

REAR AXLE SHAFT > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL REAR AXLE SHAFT OIL SEAL (w/ ABS)
Using SST and a hammer, install a new rear axle shaft oil seal.
SST
09554-22010
Seal installation depth:
34.5 to 35.5 mm (1.358 to 1.398 in.)
NOTICE:
Do not drive the oil seal in deeper than the specified range or an oil leak will occur.
Do not tilt the oil seal during installation.
Text in Illustration
*1
Rear Axle Housing Assembly
*2
Rear Axle Shaft Oil Seal
*a
Seal installation depth
Apply MP grease to the oil seal lip.
2. INSTALL REAR AXLE SHAFT OIL SEAL (w/o ABS)
Using SST and a hammer, install a new rear axle shaft oil seal.
SST
09554-22010
Seal installation depth:
0.5 to 1.5 mm (0.0197 to 0.0590 in.)
NOTICE:
Do not drive the oil seal in deeper than the specified range or an oil leak will occur.
Do not tilt the oil seal during installation.
Text in Illustration
*1
Rear Axle Housing Assembly
*2
Rear Axle Shaft Oil Seal
*a
Seal installation depth
Apply MP grease to the oil seal lip.
3. INSTALL BEARING RETAINER O-RING
Apply MP grease to the bearing retainer O-ring.
Install the bearing retainer O-ring to the rear axle shaft housing.
4. INSTALL REAR AXLE SHAFT WITH BACKING PLATE
Install the rear axle shaft with backing plate to the rear axle shaft housing with the 4 nuts.
Torque:
65 N*m{
663 kgf*cm
, 48 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Be careful not to damage the rear axle shaft oil seal.
Be careful not to damage the skid control rotor rear.
5. INSTALL REAR SPEED SENSOR (w/ ABS)
Install the new speed sensor O-ring to the rear speed sensor.
Install the rear speed sensor to the rear axle shaft housing with the bolt.
Torque:
8.0 N*m{
82 kgf*cm
, 71 in.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Be careful not to damage the rear speed sensor.
Prevent foreign matter from adhering to the speed sensor.
Do not twist the speed sensor wire when installing the speed sensor.
6. INSTALL REAR WHEEL BRAKE CYLINDER ASSEMBLY
Install the wheel brake cylinder assembly with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
9.5 N*m{
97 kgf*cm
, 84 in.*lbf
}
Using SST, connect the brake tube.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
7. APPLY HIGH TEMPERATURE GREASE
Apply high temperature grease to the shoe attached surface of the backing plate.
8. INSTALL PARKING BRAKE CABLE ASSEMBLY NO.3
Install the parking brake cable assembly No.3 to the rear brake backing plate with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
8.0 N*m{
82 kgf*cm
, 71 in.*lbf
}
9. INSTALL PARKING BRAKE SHOE STRUT SET
Apply the high temperature grease to the adjusting bolt.
Install the tension spring to the show strut set.
10. INSTALL REAR BRAKE SHOE
Using pliers, install the rear brake shoe, shoe strut set, rear cushion lever and shoe lever with a new C-washer.
Connect the parking brake cable No.3 to the front brake shoe.
Using SST, install the rear brake shoe, pin, shoe hold down spring and shoe hold down cup.
SST
09718-00010
11. INSTALL FRONT BRAKE SHOE
Install the return spring to each brake shoe.
Using SST, install the front brake shoe, pin, shoe hold down spring and shoe hold down cup.
SST
09718-00010
Install the brake shoe lower strut.
NOTICE:
Make sure that the shoe lower strut and brake rear cushion lever are securely engaged.
Using needle-nose pliers, install the tension spring.
12. CONNECT PARKING BRAKE SHOE STRUT SET
Using SST, install the upper side tension spring.
SST
09703-30011
13. INSTALL AUTOMATIC ADJUST LEVER
Install the automatic adjust lever and tension spring.
NOTICE:
Make sure to install the automatic adjust lever between the groove of the shoe strut set and brake shoe.
14. INSPECT REAR DRUM BRAKE
Check the each part is installed properly.
NOTICE:
There should be no oil or grease adhering to the friction surfaces of the shoe lining and drum.
If they are not installed correctly, reinstall them as shown in the illustration.
15. INSTALL REAR AXLE BRAKE DRUM GASKET
Install the new brake drum gasket to the rear axle shaft.
16. INSTALL REAR BRAKE DRUM SUB-ASSEMBLY
17. ADJUST REAR DRUM BRAKE SHOE CLEARANCE
Using a screwdriver from a service hole, turn the adjuster to expand the shoes so that the brake shoes touch the brake drum.
Using another screwdriver, push up the automatic adjust lever and turn the adjuster to contract the shoes so that the brake shoe does not touch the brake drum. Then turn the adjuster another 180 degrees to further contract the shoes.
Install the hole plug.
18. ADD BRAKE FLUID
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No. 116 DOT3 or equivalent
19. BLEED BRAKE LINE
Connect the vinyl tube to the bleeder plug.
Depress the brake pedal several times and loosen the bleeder plug with the pedal held down.
At the point where the fluid stops coming out, tighten the bleeder plug and release the brake pedal.
Repeat this procedure until the air in the brake fluid is completely bled out.
Tighten the bleeder plug.
Front bleeder plug:
Torque:
10.8 N*m{
110 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Rear bleeder plug:
Torque:
11.0 N*m{
112 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Repeat the above procedure to bleed the air out of the brake line for each wheel.
20. INSPECT BRAKE FLUID LEVEL
Check the fluid level and add fluid if necessary.
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No.116 DOT3 or equivalent
21. INSTALL REAR WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}
22. INSPECT PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE TRAVEL
Pull firmly on the parking brake control handle.
Release the parking brake lock, and return the parking brake control handle to its off position.
Slowly pull the parking brake control handle all the way up, and count the number of clicks.
Parking brake control handle travel:
10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
HINT:
If the number of clicks falls outside of the specified range, release the parking brake lock and adjust it.
If there are fewer clicks than the specified value, loosen the parking brake wire adjustment nut an the lock nut. With the parking brake cable loosened, adjust the clearance between the brake drum and lining.
23. ADJUST PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE TRAVEL
Lift the vehicle
Depress the parking brake handle. Hold the parking brake adjusting nut using a wrench, and loosen the lock nut.
Turn the parking brake adjusting nut until the parking brake handle travel meets the above specification.
Parking brake control handle travel:
10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
Count the number of clicks after depressing and releasing the parking brake control handle 3 or 4 times.
Check whether the parking brake drags or not.
When operating the parking brake control handle, check that the parking brake indicator light comes on.
Standard:
Brake warning light always comes on at the first click.
Hold the parking brake adjusting nut using a wrench, and tighten the lock nut.
Torque:
14.5 N*m{
148 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
Lower the vehicle.
24. CHECK ABS SPEED SENSOR SIGNAL (w/ ABS)
(
Click here
)

### REAR AXLE SHAFT > REMOVAL

REAR AXLE SHAFT > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE REAR WHEEL
2. DRAIN BRAKE FLUID
3. REMOVE REAR BRAKE DRUM SUB-ASSEMBLY
Release the parking brake lever, and remove the brake drum.
HINT:
If the brake drum cannot be removed easily, perform the following steps.
Remove the hole plug and insert a screwdriver through the hole in the backing plate, and hold the automatic adjusting lever away from the adjuster.
Using another screwdriver, contract the brake shoe adjuster by turning the adjusting wheel.
4. REMOVE REAR AXLE BRAKE DRUM GASKET
5. REMOVE AUTOMATIC ADJUST LEVER
Remove the automatic adjust lever and the tension spring.
6. SEPARATE PARKING BRAKE SHOE STRUT SET
Using SST, remove the upper side tension spring from the brake shoe.
SST
09703-30011
7. REMOVE FRONT BRAKE SHOE
Using a needle-nose pliers, remove the anchor side tension spring.
Using SST, remove the cup, shoe hold-down sprig and  pin.
SST
09718-00010
Remove the parking brake shoe lower strut.
Separate the brake shoe return spring, and remove the front brake shoe.
8. REMOVE REAR BRAKE SHOE
Using SST, remove the cup, shoe hold-down sprig and pin.
SST
09718-00010
Disconnect the parking brake cable No.3 and remove the rear brake shoe.
9. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE SHOE STRUT SET
Using a screwdriver, remove the C-washer, brake shoe lever, rear cushion lever and brake shoe strut set from the brake shoe.
Remove the adjusting bolt and tension spring from the brake shoe strut set.
10. SEPARATE PARKING BRAKE CABLE ASSEMBLY NO.3
Remove the 2 bolts and separate the parking brake cable assembly No.3 from the rear brake backing plate.
11. REMOVE REAR WHEEL BRAKE CYLINDER ASSEMBLY
Using SST, disconnect the brake tube, use a container to catch brake fluid.
SST
09023-00101
Remove the 2 bolts and wheel cylinder.
12. SEPARATE REAR SPEED SENSOR (w/ ABS)
Remove the bolt and separate the rear speed sensor.
NOTICE:
Prevent foreign matter from adhering to the speed sensor.
Be careful not to damage the speed sensor.
Remove the O-ring from the rear speed sensor.
13. REMOVE REAR AXLE SHAFT WITH BACKING PLATE
Remove the 4 nuts and rear axle shaft with backing plate.
NOTICE:
Be careful not to damage the rear axle shaft oil seal.
Be careful not to damage the skid control rotor rear.
14. REMOVE BEARING RETAINER O-RING
Remove the bearing retainer O-ring from the rear axle housing assembly.
15. REMOVE REAR AXLE SHAFT OIL SEAL
Using SST, remove the rear axle shaft oil seal from the rear axle housing assembly.
SST
09308-00010

---

### REAR AXLE SHAFT > DISASSEMBLY

REAR AXLE SHAFT > DISASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE REAR SKID CONTROL ROTOR (w/ ABS)
Temporarily install the nut to the rear brake backing plate No.1 bolt as shown in the illustration.
Using a hammer, remove the 4 bolts from the rear axle shaft bearing case.
NOTICE:
Do not reuse the nuts previously removed from the vehicle.
Grind the bearing retainer inner and skid control rotor rear surfaces using a grinder, then chisel them out with a chisel.
NOTICE:
Be careful not to damage the rear axle shaft and backing plate.
2. REMOVE REAR AXLE SHAFT SNAP RING
Using a snap ring expander, remove the rear axle shaft snap ring.
3. REMOVE REAR AXLE SHAFT
Install a washer and nut to the rear brake backing plate No.1 bolt as shown in the illustration.
Install the rear brake backing plate No.1 bolt by tightening the nut.
Position SST on the rear brake backing plate with the 4 nuts.
SST
09521-25011
Using SST and a press, remove the rear axle shaft from the rear axle shaft bearing.
NOTICE:
Be careful not to drop the rear axle shaft.
4. REMOVE REAR AXLE BEARING RETAINER INNER
Remove the rear axle bearing retainer inner.
5. REMOVE BRAKE DRUM OIL DEFLECTOR
Using SST, remove the rear axle hub bolts.
SST
09650-17011
Remove the brake drum oil deflector and brake drum oil deflector gasket.
6. REMOVE REAR AXLE SHAFT OUTER OIL SEAL
Using SST, remove the rear axle shaft outer oil seal.
SST
09308-00010
7. REMOVE REAR AXLE SHAFT BEARING
Using SST and a press, remove the rear axle shaft bearing from the rear axle shaft bearing case.
SST
09223-56010
09950-60010
(09951-00600)
8. REMOVE REAR AXLE BEARING CASE
Temporarily install the nut to the rear brake backing plate No.1 bolt as shown in the illustration.
Using a hammer, remove the 4 bolts from the rear axle shaft bearing case.
NOTICE:
Do not reuse the nuts previously removed from the vehicle.
9. INSPECT REAR AXLE SHAFT
Using a dial indicator, inspect the rear axle shaft runout.
Maximum:
1.5 mm (0.0591 in.)
NOTICE:
Ensure that the dial indicator is set at right angles to the measurement surface.
If runout exceeds the maximum, replace the rear axle shaft.
Check the rear axle shaft for damage.

---

### REAR AXLE SHAFT > REASSEMBLY

REAR AXLE SHAFT > REASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL REAR AXLE BEARING CASE
Install the rear axle bearing case to the rear brake backing plate.
Using a deep socket wrench (10 mm), socket wrench (21 mm) and press, install the 4 rear brake backing plate No.1 bolts.
2. INSTALL REAR AXLE SHAFT BEARING
Using SST and a press, install a new rear axle shaft bearing.
SST
09950-60020
(09951-00910)
3. INSTALL REAR AXLE SHAFT OUTER OIL SEAL
Using SST and a hammer, install a new rear axle shaft outer oil seal to the rear axle shaft bearing case.
SST
09223-22010
09612-30012
Apply MP grease to the oil seal lip.
4. INSTALL BRAKE DRUM OIL DEFLECTOR
Aligning the notches, install the brake drum oil deflector, brake drum oil deflector gasket and rear axle hub bolts.
Hold the rear axle shaft in a vise between aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not overtighten the vise.
Install a washer and nut to the rear axle hub bolt as shown in the illustration.
Install the rear axle hub bolt by tightening the nut.
5. INSTALL REAR AXLE SHAFT
Install the rear axle shaft sub-assembly to the rear axle shaft bearing.
Install the bearing retainer inner to the rear axle shaft bearing.
NOTICE:
Be sure to install the bearing retainer inner in the correct direction.
Using SST and a press, install the rear axle shaft sub-assembly to the rear axle shaft bearing.
SST
09316-60011
(09316-00051)
6. INSTALL REAR AXLE SHAFT SNAP RING
Using a snap ring expander, install the new rear axle shaft snap ring.
7. INSTALL SKID CONTROL ROTOR REAR (w/ ABS)
Install a new rear skid control rotor to the rear axle shaft sub-assembly, as shown in the illustration.
NOTICE:
Be sure to install the skid control rotor rear in the correct direction.
Be careful not to damage the skid control rotor rear.
Using SST and a press, install the skid control rotor rear and bearing retainer inner.
SST
09316-60011
(09316-00051)
Standard:
31 to 33 mm (1.2205 to 1.2992 in.)
NOTICE:
Be sure to install the bearing retainer inner in the correct direction.

---

### FRONT AXLE HUB > INSTALLATION

FRONT AXLE HUB > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL FRONT AXLE HUB BEARING
Using SST and a press, install a new front axle hub bearing.
SST
09649-17010
09950-70010
(09951-07100)
NOTICE:
Be careful not to damage the hub bearing.
2. INSTALL FRONT DISC
Install the front disc as shown in the illustration.
Install the front disc with the 6 bolts.
Torque:
62 N*m{
632 kgf*cm
, 46 ft.*lbf
}
3. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL ADJUSTING NUT (w/ ABS)
Install the hub nut to the front axle hub bolt.
Hold the front axle assembly in a vise between aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not overtighten the vise.
Hold the hub nut in a vise to secure the axle hub and bearing assembly.
Install the skid control rotor as shown in the illustration.
Using SST, install the front wheel adjusting nut.
SST
09607-26010
Torque:
287 N*m{
2,930 kgf*cm
, 212 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not use an extension bar to avoid applying excess torque to the nut and rotor.
Using a chisel and hammer, stake the front wheel adjusting nut.
4. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL ADJUSTING NUT (w/o ABS)
Install the hub nut to the front axle hub bolt.
Hold the front axle assembly in a vise between aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not overtighten the vise.
Hold the hub nut in a vise to secure the axle hub and bearing assembly.
Install the front wheel bearing dust deflector as shown in the illustration.
Using SST, install the front wheel adjusting nut.
SST
09607-26010
Torque:
287 N*m{
2,930 kgf*cm
, 212 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not use an extension bar to avoid applying excess torque to the nut and rotor.
Using a chisel and hammer, stake the front wheel adjusting nut.
5. INSTALL INNER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, place the steering knuckle on a press as shown in the illustration.
SST
09527-30010
Temporarily install a new inner knuckle grease retainer cap to the steering knuckle as shown in the illustration.
NOTICE:
Align the center of the cutout in the inner knuckle grease retainer cap and the groove of the steering knuckle.
Using SST and the press, press the inner knuckle grease retainer cap into the steering knuckle until the distance (A) in the illustration meets the standard value.
SST
09950-70010
(09951-07100)
09951-00900
Distance (A):
26 +/- 0.5 mm (1.02 +/- 0.0196 in.)
NOTICE:
Using a vernier caliper, measure the distance (A) at the 3 points indicated by the arrows in the illustration while pressing.
6. INSTALL FRONT LOWER BALL JOINT ASSEMBLY
Fix the steering knuckle in the vise using aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the steering knuckle.
Install the front lower ball joint assembly to the steering knuckle with the nut.
Torque:
170 N*m{
1,730 kgf*cm
, 125 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Ensure that the thread and taper are free of oil etc.
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the front lower ball joint assembly.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
7. INSTALL STEERING KNUCKLE
Install the steering knuckle and brake dust cover to the front axle hub sub-assembly with the 4 bolts.
Torque:
88 N*m{
897 kgf*cm
, 65 ft.*lbf
}
8. INSTALL OUTER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, install a new outer knuckle grease retainer cap.
SST
09950-60010
(09951-00420)
09950-70010
(09951-07150)
9. INSTALL FRONT AXLE ASSEMBLY
Temporarily install the front axle assembly to the front suspension upper arm and front suspension lower arm.
Fully tighten front suspension lower arm with the 4 bolts and 4 nuts.
Torque:
Nut (A):
90 N*m{
918 kgf*cm
, 66 ft.*lbf
}
Nut (B):
52 N*m{
530 kgf*cm
, 38 ft.*lbf
}
Fully tighten the front suspension upper arm with the nut.
Torque:
113 N*m{
1,147 kgf*cm
, 83 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the front suspension upper arm.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the castle nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
10. INSTALL TIE ROD END SUB-ASSEMBLY
Install the tie rod end sub-assembly to the steering knuckle with the castle nut.
Torque:
50 N*m{
510 kgf*cm
, 37 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the tie rod end sub-assembly.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the castle nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
11. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE CALIPER ASSEMBLY
Install the brake caliper assembly to the steering knuckle with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
123 N*m{
1,250 kgf*cm
, 91 ft.*lbf
}
12. INSTALL FRONT SPEED SENSOR (w/ ABS)
Install the speed sensor to the steering knuckle with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
8.0 N*m{
82 kgf*cm
, 71 in.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Prevent foreign matter from adhering to the speed sensor.
Be careful not to damage the speed sensor.
Do not twist the sensor wire when installing the speed sensor.
13. INSPECT FRONT AXLE HUB BEARING LOOSENESS
Using a dial indicator, check for looseness near the center of the axle hub.
Maximum:
0.07 mm (0.0028 in.)
NOTICE:
Ensure that the dial indicator is set at right angles to the measurement surface.
If looseness exceeds the maximum, replace the bearing.
14. INSPECT FRONT AXLE HUB RUNOUT
Using a dial indicator, check for runout on the surface of the axle hub outside the hub bolt.
Maximum:
0.05 mm (0.0020 in.)
NOTICE:
Ensure that the dial indicator is set at right angles to the measurement surface.
If runout exceeds the maximum, replace the axle hub.
15. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}
16. INSPECT WHEEL ALIGNMENT
(
Click here
)
17. CHECK ABS SPEED SENSOR SIGNAL
(
Click here
)

### FRONT AXLE HUB > REMOVAL

FRONT AXLE HUB > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL
2. SEPARATE FRONT SPEED SENSOR (w/ ABS)
Remove the 2 bolts, and separate the speed sensor from the steering knuckle.
NOTICE:
Be careful not to damage the speed sensor.
Prevent foreign matter from adhering to the speed sensor.
3. SEPARATE FRONT DISC BRAKE CALIPER ASSEMBLY
Remove the 2 bolts, and disconnect the brake caliper assembly.
NOTICE:
Use a wire or an equivalent to keep the brake caliper from hanging down by the flexible hose.
4. SEPARATE TIE ROD END SUB-ASSEMBLY
Remove the cotter pin and castle nut.
Using SST, separate the tie rod end from the steering knuckle.
SST
09628-00011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Do not damage the brake dust cover.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the vehicle.
5. REMOVE FRONT AXLE ASSEMBLY
Remove the 4 bolts, 4 nuts and front axle assembly from the front suspension lower arm.
Remove the cotter pin and castle nut.
Using SST, remove the front axle assembly from the front suspension upper arm.
SST
09628-62011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Apply grease to the threads and tip of the SST center bolt before use.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the vehicle.
6. REMOVE FRONT LOWER BALL JOINT ASSEMBLY
Fix the steering knuckle in the vise using aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the steering knuckle.
Remove the cotter pin.
Loosen the nut.
Using SST, separate the front lower ball joint assembly from the steering knuckle.
SST
09628-00011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the steering knuckle.
Remove the nut and front lower ball joint assembly.
7. REMOVE INNER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using a chisel, make a hole in the center of the outer knuckle grease retainer cap to insert a brass bar.
Using a brass bar, remove the inner knuckle grease retainer cap from the steering knuckle as shown in the illustration.
8. REMOVE OUTER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, remove the outer knuckle grease retainer cap.
SST
09950-60010
(09951-00300)
09950-70010
(09951-07200)
9. REMOVE STEERING KNUCKLE
Remove the 4 bolts.
Using SST, remove the steering knuckle, brake dust cover from the front axle hub sub-assembly.
SST
09950-40011
(09951-04020, 09952-04010, 09953-04010, 09955-04051)
09950-60010
(09951-00470)
NOTICE:
Apply grease to the threads and tip of the SST center bolt (09953-04010) before use.
10. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL ADJUSTING NUT (w/ ABS)
Install the hub nut to the front axle hub bolt.
Hold the axle hub and bearing assembly in a vise between aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not overtighten the vise.
Hold the hub nut in a vise to secure the axle hub and bearing assembly.
Using SST and a hammer, release the staked part of the front wheel adjusting nut.
SST
09930-00010
NOTICE:
Release the staked part of the nut completely, otherwise the threads of the front axle hub may be damaged.
Using SST, remove the front wheel adjusting nut and skid control rotor.
SST
09607-26010
NOTICE:
Do not use an extension bar to avoid applying excess torque to the nut and rotor.
11. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL ADJUSTING NUT (w/o ABS)
Install the hub nut to the front axle hub bolt.
Hold the axle hub and bearing assembly in a vise between aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not overtighten the vise.
Hold the hub nut in a vise to secure the axle hub and bearing assembly.
Using SST and a hammer, release the staked part of the front wheel adjusting nut.
SST
09930-00010
NOTICE:
Release the staked part of the nut completely, otherwise the threads of the front axle hub may be damaged.
Using SST, remove the front wheel adjusting nut and front wheel bearing dust deflector No.1.
SST
09607-26010
NOTICE:
Do not use an extension bar to avoid applying excess torque to the nut and rotor.
12. REMOVE FRONT DISC
Remove the 6 bolts.
Remove the front disc as shown in the illustration.
13. REMOVE FRONT AXLE HUB BEARING
Using SST, remove the front axle hub bearing from the front axle hub sub-assembly.
SST
09950-40011
(09951-04020, 09952-04010, 09953-04020, 09954-04010, 09955-04031, 09957-04010, 09958-04011)
09950-60010
(09951-00360, 09951-00480, 09952-06010)
NOTICE:
Apply a small amount of grease to the threads and tip of SST (center bolt) before use.
Using SST, remove the front axle hub bearing (inner race) from the front axle hub sub-assembly.
SST
09950-00020
09950-00030
09950-40011
(09957-04010)
09950-60010
(09951-00360, 09951-00480, 09952-06010)
NOTICE:
Apply a small amount of grease to the threads and tip of SST (center bolt) before use.

---

### REAR SHOCK ABSORBER > DISPOSAL

REAR SHOCK ABSORBER > DISPOSAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. DISPOSE OF SHOCK ABSORBER ASSEMBLY REAR
Fully extend the shock absorber rod.
Using a drill, make a hole in the cylinder somewhere between A and B, as shown in the illustration, to discharge the gas inside.
CAUTION:
Be careful of flying metal shards when drilling. Always use proper safety equipment.
The gas is colorless, odorless and non-poisonous.

---

### REAR SHOCK ABSORBER > INSTALLATION

REAR SHOCK ABSORBER > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. TEMPORARILY TIGHTEN REAR SHOCK ABSORBER ASSEMBLY
Install the 2 rear shock absorber bushings on the rear shock absorber.
NOTICE:
Be careful of size differences between the upper and lower rear shock absorber bushings.
HINT:
If the rear shock absorber bushing is separated, install it as shown in the illustration.
Install the cushion washer No.2.
Temporarily tighten the upper side of the rear shock absorber assembly with the bolt.
Install the cushion washer No.4.
Temporarily tighten the lower side of the rear shock absorber assembly with the bolt.
2. STABILIZE SUSPENSION
After lowering the vehicle, bounce the vehicle up and down to stabilize the rear suspension.
3. FULLY TIGHTEN REAR SHOCK ABSORBER ASSEMBLY
Fully tighten the bolt on the upper side.
Torque:
24 N*m{
245 kgf*cm
, 18 ft.*lbf
}
Fully tighten the bolt on the lower side.
Torque:
43 N*m{
440 kgf*cm
, 32 ft.*lbf
}

---

### REAR SHOCK ABSORBER > REMOVAL

REAR SHOCK ABSORBER > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE REAR SHOCK ABSORBER ASSEMBLY
Support the rear axle housing assembly with a jack using a wooden block to avoid damage.
Remove the bolt and separate the rear shock absorber assembly.
Remove the cushion washer No.4.
Remove the bolt and rear shock absorber assembly.
Remove the cushion washer No.2.
Remove the 2 rear shock absorber bushings from the rear shock absorber assembly.

---

### STEERING KNUCKLE > INSTALLATION

STEERING KNUCKLE > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL INNER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, place the steering knuckle on a press as shown in the illustration.
SST
09527-30010
Temporarily install a new inner knuckle grease retainer cap to the steering knuckle as shown in the illustration.
NOTICE:
Align the center of the cutout in the inner knuckle grease retainer cap and the groove of the steering knuckle.
Using SST and the press, press the inner knuckle grease retainer cap into the steering knuckle until the distance (A) in the illustration meets the standard value.
SST
09950-70010
(09951-07100)
09951-00900
Distance (A):
26 +/- 0.5 mm (1.02 +/- 0.0196 in.)
NOTICE:
Using a vernier caliper, measure the distance (A) at the 3 points indicated by the arrows in the illustration while pressing.
2. INSTALL FRONT LOWER BALL JOINT ASSEMBLY
Fix the steering knuckle in the vise using aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the steering knuckle.
Install the front lower ball joint assembly to the steering knuckle with the nut.
Torque:
170 N*m{
1,730 kgf*cm
, 125 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Ensure that the thread and taper are free of oil etc.
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the front lower ball joint assembly.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
3. INSTALL STEERING KNUCKLE
Install the steering knuckle and brake dust cover to the front axle hub sub-assembly with the 4 bolts.
Torque:
88 N*m{
897 kgf*cm
, 65 ft.*lbf
}
4. INSTALL OUTER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, install a new outer knuckle grease retainer cap.
SST
09950-60010
(09951-00420)
09950-70010
(09951-07150)
5. INSTALL FRONT AXLE ASSEMBLY
Temporarily install the front axle assembly to the front suspension upper arm and front suspension lower arm.
Fully tighten front suspension lower arm with the 4 bolts and 4 nuts.
Torque:
Nut (A):
90 N*m{
918 kgf*cm
, 66 ft.*lbf
}
Nut (B):
52 N*m{
530 kgf*cm
, 38 ft.*lbf
}
Fully tighten the front suspension upper arm with the nut.
Torque:
113 N*m{
1,147 kgf*cm
, 83 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the front suspension upper arm.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the castle nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
6. INSTALL TIE ROD END SUB-ASSEMBLY
Install the tie rod end sub-assembly to the steering knuckle with the castle nut.
Torque:
50 N*m{
510 kgf*cm
, 37 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the tie rod end sub-assembly.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the castle nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
7. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE CALIPER ASSEMBLY
Install the brake caliper assembly to the steering knuckle with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
123 N*m{
1,250 kgf*cm
, 91 ft.*lbf
}
8. INSTALL FRONT SPEED SENSOR (w/ ABS)
Install the speed sensor to the steering knuckle with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
8.0 N*m{
82 kgf*cm
, 71 in.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Prevent foreign matter from adhering to the speed sensor.
Be careful not to damage the speed sensor.
Do not twist the sensor wire when installing the speed sensor.
9. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}
10. INSPECT WHEEL ALIGNMENT
(
Click here
)
11. CHECK ABS SPEED SENSOR SIGNAL (w/ ABS)
(
Click here
)

### STEERING KNUCKLE > REMOVAL

STEERING KNUCKLE > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL
2. SEPARATE FRONT SPEED SENSOR (w/ ABS)
Remove the 2 bolts, and separate the speed sensor from the steering knuckle.
NOTICE:
Be careful not to damage the speed sensor.
Prevent foreign matter from adhering to the speed sensor.
3. SEPARATE FRONT DISC BRAKE CALIPER ASSEMBLY
Remove the 2 bolts, and disconnect the brake caliper assembly.
NOTICE:
Use a wire or an equivalent to keep the brake caliper from hanging down by the flexible hose.
4. SEPARATE TIE ROD END SUB-ASSEMBLY
Remove the cotter pin and castle nut.
Using SST, separate the tie rod end from the steering knuckle.
SST
09628-00011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Do not damage the brake dust cover.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the vehicle.
5. REMOVE FRONT AXLE ASSEMBLY
Remove the 4 bolts, 4 nuts and front axle assembly from the front suspension lower arm.
Remove the cotter pin and castle nut.
Using SST, remove the front axle assembly from the front suspension upper arm.
SST
09628-62011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Apply grease to the threads and tip of the SST center bolt before use.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the vehicle.
6. REMOVE FRONT LOWER BALL JOINT ASSEMBLY
Fix the steering knuckle in the vise using aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the steering knuckle.
Remove the cotter pin.
Loosen the nut.
Using SST, separate the front lower ball joint assembly from the steering knuckle.
SST
09628-00011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the steering knuckle.
Remove the nut and front lower ball joint assembly.
7. REMOVE INNER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using a chisel, make a hole in the center of the outer knuckle grease retainer cap to insert a brass bar.
Using a brass bar, remove the inner knuckle grease retainer cap from the steering knuckle as shown in the illustration.
8. REMOVE OUTER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, remove the outer knuckle grease retainer cap.
SST
09950-60010
(09951-00300)
09950-70010
(09951-07200)
9. REMOVE STEERING KNUCKLE
Remove the 4 bolts.
Using SST, remove the steering knuckle, brake dust cover from the front axle hub sub-assembly.
SST
09950-40011
(09951-04020, 09952-04010, 09953-04010, 09955-04051)
09950-60010
(09951-00470)
NOTICE:
Apply grease to the threads and tip of the SST center bolt (09953-04010) before use.

### FRONT BRAKE FLEXIBLE HOSE > INSTALLATION

FRONT BRAKE FLEXIBLE HOSE > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL FRONT FLEXIBLE HOSE
Connect the front brake flexible hose with the union bolt and a new gasket.
Torque:
30 N*m{
310 kgf*cm
, 22 ft.*lbf
}
Install the front brake flexible hose with a new clip.
NOTICE:
When installing the front brake flexible hose, face the identification mark to the exterior of the vehicle and minimize twisting of the hose.
Install the clip as far as it will go.
Connect the brake tube to the front brake flexible hose with a SST while holding flexible hose with a wrench.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not bend or damage the brake tube.
Do not allow any foreign matter such as dirt and dust to enter the brake tube from the connecting points.
2. ADD BRAKE FLUID
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No. 116 DOT3 or equivalent
3. BLEED BRAKE LINE
Connect the vinyl tube to the bleeder plug.
Depress the brake pedal several times and loosen the bleeder plug with the pedal held down.
At the point where the fluid stops coming out, tighten the bleeder plug and release the brake pedal.
Repeat this procedure until the air in the brake fluid is completely bled out.
Tighten the bleeder plug.
Front bleeder plug:
Torque:
10.8 N*m{
110 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Rear bleeder plug:
Torque:
11.0 N*m{
112 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Repeat the above procedure to bleed the air out of the brake line for each wheel.
4. INSPECT BRAKE FLUID LEVEL IN RESERVOIR
Check the fluid level and add fluid if necessary.
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No.116 DOT3 or equivalent
5. CHECK BRAKE FLUID LEAKAGE
6. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}

---

FRONT BRAKE FLEXIBLE HOSE > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL
2. DRAIN BRAKE FLUID
3. REMOVE FRONT FLEXIBLE HOSE
Remove the brake tube with SST while holding the front brake flexible hose with a wrench.
SST
09023-00101
NOTICE:
Do not bend or damage the brake tube.
Do not allow any foreign matter such as dirt and dust to enter the brake tube from the connecting points.
Remove the clip.
Remove the union bolt, the front brake flexible hose and gasket.

### STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > INSTALLATION

STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL WIPER SWITCH
Install the wiper switch with the claw.
HINT:
Slide the steering column cover upper upward to install the wiper switch.
Connect the connector.
2. INSTALL HEADLIGHT DIMMER SWITCH
Install the headlight dimmer switch with the claw.
HINT:
Slide the steering column upper cover upward to install the headlight dimmer switch.
Connect the connector.
3. INSTALL SPIRAL CABLE ASSEMBLY (W/ AIRBAG)
Check that the front wheels are facing straight ahead.
Set the turn signal switch to the neutral position.
NOTICE:
If it is not in the neutral position, the pin of the turn signal switch may snap.
Engage the 3 claws and install the spiral cable with steering sensor.
NOTICE:
When replacing the spiral cable with a new one, remove the lock pin before installing the steering wheel assembly.
Connect the connectors to the spiral cable with steering sensor.
NOTICE:
When handling the airbag connector, take care not to damage the airbag wire harness.
4. INSTALL STEERING COLUMN COVER UPPER
5. INSTALL HORN CONTACT PLATE (W/O AIRBAG)
Engage the 2 claws and install the horn contact plate.
Connect the connector.
6. INSTALL STEERING INTERMEDIATE SHAFT
Align the matchmarks on the steering intermediate shaft assembly No.2 and the steering gear assembly.
Temporarily install the bolt.
7. INSTALL STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY
Temporarily install the steering column assembly with the 3 bolts.
Fully tighten the 3 bolts on the steering column assembly.
Torque:
21 N*m{
214 kgf*cm
, 16 ft.*lbf
}
Fully tighten the bolt on the steering intermediate shaft assembly No.2.
Torque:
36 N*m{
367 kgf*cm
, 27 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Ensure that the steering column assembly is in the lowermost tilt position.
Connect the connectors and clamp the wire harness to the steering column assembly bracket.
Install the steering shaft cover with the 3 bolts.
Torque:
8.0 N*m{
82 kgf*cm
, 71 in.*lbf
}
8. INSTALL INSTRUMENT PANEL FINISH PANEL LOWER
Engage the 4 clips (B).
Install the instrument panel finish panel lower with the 2 clips (A).
9. PLACE FRONT WHEELS FACING STRAIGHT AHEAD
10. CENTER SPIRAL CABLE (W/ AIRBAG)
Check that the ignition switch is off.
Check that the battery negative (-) terminal is disconnected.
CAUTION:
After removing the terminal, wait for at least 90 seconds before starting the operation.
Rotate the spiral cable with steering sensor counterclockwise slowly by hand until it feels firm.
NOTICE:
Do not turn the spiral cable with steering sensor by the airbag wire harness.
Rotate the spiral cable with steering sensor clockwise approximately 2.5 turns to align the marks.
NOTICE:
Do not turn the spiral cable with steering sensor by the airbag wire harness.
HINT:
The spiral cable with steering sensor will rotate approximately 2.5 turns to both the left and right from the center.
11. INSTALL STEERING COLUMN COVER LOWER
Engage the 3 claws to install the steering column cover upper and lower with the 2 "torx" screws.
Torque:
1.5 N*m{
15 kgf*cm
, 13 in.*lbf
}
12. INSTALL STEERING WHEEL ASSEMBLY
w/o AIRBAG:
Position the horn contact plate so that the projecting parts are as shown the illustration.
Align the matchmarks on the steering wheel assembly and the steering column assembly.
Install the nut.
Torque:
50 N*m{
510 kgf*cm
, 37 ft.*lbf
}
Connect the connectors.
13. INSPECT STEERING WHEEL CENTER POINT
14. INSTALL STEERING PAD (W/ AIRBAG)
Support the steering pad with one hand.
Connect the connector to the steering pad.
NOTICE:
When handling the airbag connector, take care not to damage the airbag wire harness.
Connect the horn connector.
Confirm that the circumference groove of the "torx" screw fits in the screw case, and place the steering pad onto the steering wheel assembly.
Using a "torx" socket wrench (T30), tighten the 2 "torx" screws.
Torque:
8.8 N*m{
90 kgf*cm
, 78 in.*lbf
}
15. INSTALL STEERING WHEEL COVER LOWER NO.3 (W/ AIRBAG)
Install the steering wheel cover lower No.3.
16. INSTALL STEERING WHEEL COVER LOWER NO.2 (W/ AIRBAG)
Install the steering wheel cover lower No.2.
17. INSPECT STEERING PAD (W/ AIRBAG)
With the steering pad installed on the vehicle, perform a visual check. If there are any defects as mentioned below, replace the steering pad with a new one:
Cuts, minute cracks or marked discoloration on the steering pad top surface or in the grooved portion.
Make sure that the horn sounds.
HINT:
If the horn does not sound, inspect the horn system.
18. INSTALL STEERING PAD (W/O AIRBAG)
Connect the horn connector.
Install the steering pad with the screw.
Torque:
1.5 N*m{
15 kgf*cm
, 13 in.*lbf
}
19. CONNECT CABLE TO BATTERY NEGATIVE TERMINAL
20. INSPECT SUPPLEMENTAL RESTRAINT SYSTEM WARNING LIGHT
(
Click here
)
21. PERFORM INITIALIZATION
Some system need initialization when reconnecting battery cable. (
Click here
)

---

### STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > INSPECTION

STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > INSPECTION
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE STEERING COLUMN TUBE ASSEMBLY
Check that the steering column tube bearing rotates smoothly without looseness (in the axial direction) and that there is no irregular noise.
HINT:
Replace the steering column assembly if the bearing does not rotate as specified above.

---

### STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > REMOVAL

STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. DISCONNECT CABLE FROM BATTERY NEGATIVE TERMINAL
NOTICE:
Wait for 90 seconds after disconnecting the cable to prevent the airbag working.
2. PLACE FRONT WHEELS FACING STRAIGHT AHEAD
3. REMOVE STEERING PAD (W/O AIRBAG)
Remove the screw.
Remove the steering pad.
Disconnect the horn connector.
4. REMOVE STEERING WHEEL COVER LOWER NO.2 (W/ AIRBAG)
Using a screwdriver, remove the steering wheel No.2 cover lower.
HINT:
Tape up the screwdriver tip before use.
5. REMOVE STEERING WHEEL COVER LOWER NO.3 (W/ AIRBAG)
Using a screwdriver, remove the steering wheel No.3 cover lower.
HINT:
Tape up the screwdriver tip before use.
6. REMOVE STEERING PAD (W/ AIRBAG)
Using a "torx" socket wrench (T30), loosen the 2 "torx" screws until the groove along the screw circumference catches on the screw case.
Pull out the steering pad from the steering wheel assembly and support the steering pad with one hand.
Disconnect the horn connector.
Disconnect the connector and remove the steering pad.
NOTICE:
When handling the airbag connector, take care not to damage the airbag wire harness.
7. REMOVE STEERING WHEEL ASSEMBLY
Remove the steering wheel set nut.
Put matchmarks on the steering wheel assembly and steering main shaft assembly.
Using SST, remove the steering wheel assembly.
SST
09950-50013
(09951-05010, 09952-05010, 09953-05020, 09954-05021)
8. REMOVE STEERING COLUMN COVER LOWER
Set the steering tilt mechanism to the lowermost position.
Remove the 2 "torx" screws from the steering column cover lower.
Press on the left side of the steering column cover lower to disengage claw A.
Press on the right side of the steering column cover lower to disengage claw B.
HINT:
If it is difficult to disengage claw B, enlarge the clearance downward on the left side of the steering column cover lower to disengage the claw.
Twist the steering column cover lower by pulling it downward.
NOTICE:
Be sure to pull the steering column cover lower downward to prevent damage to the claw.
9. REMOVE INSTRUMENT PANEL FINISH PANEL LOWER
Using a clip remover, remove the 2 clips (A).
Disengage the 4 clips (B).
Disconnect the connector.
Push the positions indicated by the arrows in the illustration to separate the fuel lid lock control cable and bonnet (hood) control cable assembly from the panel.
Remove the instrument panel finish lower.
10. REMOVE STEERING INTERMEDIATE SHAFT
Remove the 3 bolts and the steering shaft cover.
Remove the bolt.
Slide the steering intermediate shaft assembly No.2 and put matchmarks on the steering gear assembly and the steering intermediate shaft assembly No.2.
11. REMOVE STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY
Disconnect the connectors and wire harness clamps from the steering column assembly.
Remove the 3 bolts and the steering column assembly.
12. REMOVE HORN CONTACT PLATE (W/O AIRBAG)
Disengage the 3 claws and remove the horn contact plate.
13. REMOVE STEERING COLUMN COVER UPPER
14. REMOVE SPIRAL CABLE ASSEMBLY (W/ AIRBAG)
Lock the shift lever at the lowest position.
Disengage the claws and slide the steering column upper cover upward.
HINT:
Do not remove the steering column upper cover.
Disconnect the connectors from the spiral cable.
Disengage the 3 claws and remove the spiral cable.
15. REMOVE WIPER SWITCH
Disconnect the connector.
Disengage the claw and remove the wiper switch as shown in the illustration.
NOTICE:
If the claw is pushed with excessive force, it may be broken.
HINT:
Slide the steering column cover upper upward to remove the wiper switch.
16. REMOVE HEADLIGHT DIMMER SWITCH
Disconnect the connector.
Disengage the claw and remove the headlight dimmer switch as shown in the illustration.
NOTICE:
If the claw is pushed with excessive force, it may break.
HINT:
Slide the steering column upper cover upward to remove the headlight dimmer switch.

---

### STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > DISASSEMBLY

STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > DISASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE STEERING INTERMEDIATE SHAFT NO.2
Put matchmarks on the steering main shaft assembly and the steering intermediate shaft assembly No.2.
Remove the 2 bolts and the steering intermediate shaft assembly No.2 from the steering main shaft assembly.
2. REMOVE STEERING COLUMN UPPER W/ SWITCH BRACKET ASSEMBLY
Using a center punch, mark the center of the 2 tapered bolts.
Using a drill, drill a hole in the 2 bolts.
Using a screw extractor, remove the 2 bolts and the steering column upper with switch bracket assembly.
3. REMOVE STEERING MAIN SHAFT ASSEMBLY
Secure the steering column tube in a vise. Insert aluminum plates between the steering column tube and the vise to prevent damage.
NOTICE:
Do not overtighten the vise.
Using a snap ring expander, remove the steering main shaft snap ring (outer side).
Using a brass bar and a hammer, remove the steering main shaft assembly and steering main shaft bearing.
NOTICE:
Be careful not to drop the steering main shaft assembly.
Using a snap ring expander, remove the steering main shaft snap ring (inner side).
4. REMOVE KEY CYLINDER LIGHT ASSEMBLY
Disengage the 2 claws and remove the key cylinder light assembly from the the steering column upper with switch bracket assembly.
5. REMOVE IGNITION SWITCH LOCK CYLINDER ASSEMBLY
Place the ignition switch lock cylinder assembly in the ACC position.
Push down the stop pin with a screwdriver, and pull out the cylinder assembly.
6. REMOVE UN-LOCK WARNING SWITCH ASSEMBLY
Manual transmission:
Cut the clamp securing the wire harness of the un-lock warning switch assembly.
Separate the un-lock warning switch assembly connector from the ignition or starter switch assembly.
Remove the un-lock warning switch assembly by pushing up on the center part and disengaging the 2 claws.
Automatic transmission:
Remove the 2 screws and the key inter lock solenoid from the steering column upper with switch bracket assembly.
Release the lock inside the connector, pull the terminals out, and remove the key inter lock solenoid.
7. REMOVE IGNITION OR STARTER SWITCH ASSEMBLY
Remove the 2 screws and the ignition or starter switch assembly from the steering column upper bracket assembly.
8. REMOVE STEERING COLUMN CLAMP UPPER NO.2

---

### STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > REASSEMBLY

STEERING COLUMN ASSEMBLY > REASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL STEERING COLUMN CLAMP UPPER NO.2
2. INSTALL IGNITION OR STARTER SWITCH ASSEMBLY
Install the ignition or starter switch assembly to the steering column upper bracket assembly with the 2 screws.
3. INSTALL UN-LOCK WARNING SWITCH ASSEMBLY
Automatic transmission:
Connect the key interlock solenoid terminals to the un-lock warning switch assembly connector.
Slide the un-lock warning switch assembly and engage the 2 claws to install the assembly to the steering column upper with switch bracket assembly.
Connect the un-lock warning switch assembly connector to the ignition or starter switch assembly.
Manual transmission:
Secure the un-lock warning switch assembly wire harness to the steering column upper bracket assembly with a new clamp as shown in the illustration.
NOTICE:
Do not overtighten the clamp when securing the wire harness as the wire harness could be cut.
Automatic transmission:
Install the key inter lock solenoid to the steering column upper bracket assembly with the 2 screws.
4. INSTALL IGNITION SWITCH LOCK CYLINDER ASSEMBLY
Make sure the ignition switch lock cylinder assembly is in the ACC position.
Install the ignition switch lock cylinder assembly to the steering column upper with switch bracket assembly.
5. INSPECT STEERING LOCK OPERATION
Check that the steering lock mechanism is activated when the key is removed.
Check that the steering lock mechanism is deactivated when the key is inserted and turned to the ACC position.
HINT:
If there is any abnormality, replace the ignition switch lock cylinder assembly.
6. INSTALL STEERING MAIN SHAFT ASSEMBLY
Using a snap ring expander, install a new steering main shaft snap ring (inner side) to the steering main shaft assembly.
Install the steering main shaft assembly to the steering column tube.
Secure the steering column tube in a vise. Insert aluminum plates between the steering column tube and the vise to prevent damage.
NOTICE:
Do not overtighten the vise.
Using a snap ring expander, install a new steering main shaft snap ring (outer side) to the steering main shaft assembly.
7. INSTALL STEERING MAIN SHAFT BEARING
Hold the steering column tube in a vise between aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not apply excessive force to the steering column tube bracket when holding the steering column tube in a vise.
Install a new steering main shaft bearing to the steering column tube using SST and a hammer.
SST
09612-22011
8. INSTALL STEERING COLUMN UPPER W/ SWITCH BRACKET ASSEMBLY
Temporarily install the steering column upper with switch bracket assembly and steering column upper clamp with 2 new tapered-head bolts.
Tighten the 2 tapered-head bolts until the bolt heads break off.
9. INSTALL KEY CYLINDER LIGHT ASSEMBLY
Engage the 2 claws and install the key cylinder light assembly to the steering column upper bracket assembly.
10. INSTALL STEERING INTERMEDIATE SHAFT NO.2
Align the matchmarks on the intermediate shaft assembly No.2 and the steering main shaft assembly.
Install the bolt.
Torque:
36 N*m{
367 kgf*cm
, 27 ft.*lbf
}

### PARKING BRAKE SYSTEM > ADJUSTMENT

PARKING BRAKE SYSTEM > ADJUSTMENT
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSPECT PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE TRAVEL
Pull firmly on the parking brake control handle.
Release the parking brake lock, and return the parking brake control handle to its off position.
Slowly pull the parking brake control handle all the way up, and count the number of clicks.
Parking brake control handle travel:
10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
HINT:
If the number of clicks falls outside of the specified range, release the parking brake lock and adjust it.
If there are fewer clicks than the specified value, loosen the parking brake wire adjustment nut an the lock nut. With the parking brake cable loosened, adjust the clearance between the brake drum and lining.
2. REMOVE REAR WHEEL
3. ADJUST REAR BRAKE SHOE CLEARANCE
4. INSTALL REAR WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}
5. ADJUST PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE TRAVEL
Lift the vehicle
Depress the parking brake handle. Hold the parking brake adjusting nut using a wrench, and loosen the lock nut.
Turn the parking brake adjusting nut until the parking brake handle travel meets the above specification.
Parking brake control handle travel:
10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
Count the number of clicks after depressing and releasing the parking brake control handle 3 or 4 times.
Check whether the parking brake drags or not.
When operating the parking brake control handle, check that the parking brake indicator light comes on.
Standard:
Brake warning light always comes on at the first click.
Hold the parking brake adjusting nut using a wrench, and tighten the lock nut.
Torque:
14.5 N*m{
148 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
Lower the vehicle.

for Preparation

, 15 ft.*lbf
}

, 75 in.*lbf
}

20 N*m{

, 15 ft.*lbf
}

Return the parking brake control handle to its initial position.
Install the parking brake control handle with the 2 bolts.
Torque:

}

Install the parking brake cable assembly No.1 to the body with the 4 bolts. (Procedure A)
Torque:
14.5 N*m{

, 11 ft.*lbf
}
3. REMOVE INSTRUMENT PANEL TO FLOOR BRACE SUB-ASSEMBLY (LHD)

Engage the 6 claws and install the parking brake hole cover.
5. INSTALL INSTRUMENT PANEL UNDER COVER ASSEMBLY NO.1
Engage the 3 claws.
Install the instrument panel No.1 under cover sub-assembly with the 2 clips.
6. INSTALL INSTRUMENT PANEL FINISH PANEL LOWER
Engage the 4 clips (B).
Install the instrument panel finish panel lower with the 2 clips (A).
7. INSTALL ENGINE UNDER COVER NO.2
8. INSTALL ENGINE UNDER COVER NO.1
9. INSPECT PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE TRAVEL
Pull firmly on the parking brake control handle.
Release the parking brake lock, and return the parking brake control handle to its off position.
Slowly pull the parking brake control handle all the way up, and count the number of clicks.
Parking brake control handle travel:
10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
HINT:
If the number of clicks falls outside of the specified range, release the parking brake lock and adjust it.
If there are fewer clicks than the specified value, loosen the parking brake wire adjustment nut an the lock nut. With the parking brake cable loosened, adjust the clearance between the brake drum and lining.
10. REMOVE REAR WHEEL
11. ADJUST REAR BRAKE SHOE CLEARANCE

}

Turn the parking brake adjusting nut until the parking brake handle travel meets the above specification.
Parking brake control handle travel:
10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
Count the number of clicks after depressing and releasing the parking brake control handle 3 or 4 times.
Check whether the parking brake drags or not.
When operating the parking brake control handle, check that the parking brake indicator light comes on.
Standard:
Brake warning light always comes on at the first click.
Hold the parking brake adjusting nut using a wrench, and tighten the lock nut.

, 11 ft.*lbf

### PARKING BRAKE LEVER > REMOVAL

PARKING BRAKE LEVER > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE ENGINE UNDER COVER NO.1
2. REMOVE ENGINE UNDER COVER NO.2
3. REMOVE INSTRUMENT PANEL FINISH PANEL LOWER
Using a clip remover, remove the 2 clips (A).
Disengage the 4 clips (B).
Disconnect the connector.
Push the positions indicated by the arrows in the illustration to separate the fuel lid lock control cable and bonnet (hood) control cable assembly from the panel.
Remove the instrument panel finish lower.
4. REMOVE INSTRUMENT PANEL UNDER COVER ASSEMBLY NO.1
Using a clip remover, remove the 2 clips
Disengage the 3 claws and remove the instrument panel No.1 under cover sub-assembly.
5. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE HOLE COVER
Disengage the 6 claws and remove the parking brake hole cover.
6. REMOVE INSTRUMENT PANEL TO FLOOR BRACE SUB-ASSEMBLY (LHD)
Remove the 5 bolts and the instrument panel to floor brace sub-assembly.
7. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE ASSEMBLY (LHD)
Release the parking brake control handle.
Remove the 2 bolts.
Disconnect the parking brake switch connector.
Lower the parking brake control handle.
Remove the 2 nuts, and separate the brake control handle assembly from body.
Remove the 4 bolts, and separate the parking brake cable assembly No.1 from the body. (Procedure A)
Separate the parking brake cable assembly No.1 from the parking brake equalizer. (Procedure B)
Remove the clamps.
Remove the 2 bolts and separate the parking brake cable assembly No.1 from the body.
Pull the parking brake cable assembly No.1 into the cabin and remove the parking brake control handle.
8. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE ASSEMBLY (RHD)
Release the parking brake control handle.
Remove the 2 bolts.
Disconnect the parking brake switch connector.
Lower the parking brake control handle.
Remove the 2 nuts, and separate the parking brake control handle assembly.
Remove the 4 bolts, and separate the parking brake cable assembly No.1 from the body. (Procedure A)
Separate the parking brake cable assembly No.1 from the parking brake equalizer. (Procedure B)
Remove the clamps.
Remove the 2 bolts and separate the parking brake cable assembly No.1 from the body.
Pull the parking brake cable assembly No.1 into the cabin and remove it from the driver side together with the parking brake control handle.

### PARKING BRAKE LEVER > DISASSEMBLY

PARKING BRAKE LEVER > DISASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE CABLE ASSEMBLY NO.1
Remove the parking brake plunger protector.
Remove the clip.
Remove the clip from the parking brake wire hole pin.
Remove the parking brake wire hole pin and the parking brake wire pulley.
Remove the parking brake switch assembly.
Move the parking brake pawl (A) to release the lock, and push in the parking brake plunger assembly (B) as shown in the illustration.
Separate the parking brake cable assembly No.1 from the parking brake plunger assembly.
Remove the parking brake cable assembly No.1 from the bracket.
2. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE PLUNGER ASSEMBLY
Using a punch (φ3 mm) and hammer, remove the parking brake plunger pin.
Using a small screwdriver, remove the parking brake plunger anti-rattle.
HINT:
Turn the parking brake anti-rattle, and remove it from its end.
Tape up the screwdriver tip before use.
Remove the parking brake plunger assembly from the parking brake plunger guide.
Using a small screwdriver, remove the parking brake plunger bushing.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the plunger guide.
HINT:
Tape up the screwdriver tip before use.
Using a drill (φ4mm), remove the staking part of the parking brake pawl pin.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the plunger guide.
Remove the 2 parking brake pawl pins, 2 parking brake pawls and 2 springs.

---

### PARKING BRAKE LEVER > REASSEMBLY

PARKING BRAKE LEVER > REASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL PARKING BRAKE PLUNGER ASSEMBLY
Apply MP grease to 2 new parking brake pawls and 2 pins.
Install the 2 parking brake pawls and springs to the parking brake plunger guide.
Install the 2 parking brake pawl pins.
Holding one side of the parking brake pawl pin with a metal block, tap the other side with a hammer to stake it.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the plunger guide.
Using a screwdriver, install a new parking brake bush to the parking brake plunger guide.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the plunger guide.
HINT:
Tape up the screwdriver tip before use.
Move the 2 parking brake pawls (A) to release the lock, and push in the parking brake plunger assembly (B) as shown in the illustration.
Install a new parking brake plunger anti-rattle to the parking brake plunger assembly.
Using a hammer, install the parking brake plunger pin in the position shown in the illustration.
NOTICE:
Install the plunger pin parallel to the parking brake switch bracket.
2. INSTALL PARKING BRAKE CABLE ASSEMBLY NO.1
Install the parking brake cable assembly No.1 to the bracket.
Move the parking brake pawl (A) to release the lock, and push in the parking brake plunger assembly as shown in the illustration (B).
Connect the parking brake cable assembly No.1 to the parking brake plunger assembly.
Install the bracket to the parking brake plunger guide.
Apply MP grease to the parking brake wire pawl pin.
Push the parking brake wire pulley as shown in the illustration, install the parking brake wire pawl pin.
Install the clip to the parking brake wire pawl pin.
Install the clip.
Install the parking brake switch.
Torque:
8.8 N*m{
90 kgf*cm
, 78 in.*lbf
}
Install the parking brake plunger protector.

---

10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
HINT:
If the number of clicks falls outside of the specified range, release the parking brake lock and adjust it.

}

Count the number of clicks after depressing and releasing the parking brake control handle 3 or 4 times.

### PARKING BRAKE CABLE > REMOVAL

PARKING BRAKE CABLE > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE REAR WHEEL
2. REMOVE REAR BRAKE DRUM
Release the parking brake lever, and remove the brake drum.
HINT:
If the brake drum cannot be removed easily, perform the following steps.

09703-30011

Using SST, remove the cup, shoe hold-down sprig and  pin.
SST
09718-00010
Remove the parking brake shoe lower strut.
Separate the brake shoe return spring, and remove the front brake shoe.
7. REMOVE REAR BRAKE SHOE
Using SST, remove the cup, shoe hold-down sprig and pin.
SST
09718-00010
Disconnect the parking brake cable No.3 and remove the rear brake shoe.
8. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE CABLE ASSEMBLY NO.3 (NARROW BODY)
Remove the 2 bolts and separate the parking brake cable assembly No.3 from the backing plate.

9. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE CABLE ASSEMBLY NO.3 (WIDE BODY)

Remove the 6 bolts, nut and parking brake cable assembly No.3.
Remove the parking brake cable assembly No.3 from the parking brake equalizer.

### STEERING PAD > DISPOSAL

STEERING PAD > DISPOSAL
for Preparation
Click here
HINT:
When scrapping a vehicle equipped with the SRS or disposing of the steering pad, be sure to deploy the airbag first in accordance with the procedure described below. If any abnormality occurs with the airbag deployment, contact the SERVICE DEPT. of the DISTRIBUTOR.
CAUTION:
Never dispose of a steering pad that has an undeployed airbag.
The airbag produces an exploding sound when it is deployed, so perform the operation outdoors and where it will not create a nuisance to nearby residents.
When deploying the airbag, always use the specified SST (SRS Airbag Deployment Tool). Perform the operation in a place away from electrical noise.
When deploying the airbag, perform the operation at least 10 m (33 ft) away from the steering pad.
The steering pad becomes extremely hot when the airbag is deployed, so do not touch it for at least 30 minutes after deployment.
Use gloves and safety glasses when handling a steering pad with a deployed airbag.
Do not apply water, etc. to a steering pad with a deployed airbag.
Always wash your hands with water after completing the operation.
1. DISPOSE OF HORN BUTTON ASSEMBLY (WHEN INSTALLED IN VEHICLE)
HINT:
Prepare a battery as the power source to deploy the airbag.
Check the function of the SST.
SST
09082-00700
CAUTION:
When deploying the airbag, always use the specified SST:
SRS Airbag Deployment Tool.
Connect the SST to the battery.
Connect the red clip of the SST to the battery positive (+) terminal and the black clip of the SST to the battery negative (-) terminal.
Check the function of the SST.
Press the SST activation switch, and check that the LED of the SST activation switch comes on.
CAUTION:
Do not connect the SST connector (yellow colored one) to the airbag.
If the LED comes on when the activation switch is not being pressed, SST malfunction is possible, so replace the SST with a new one.
Disconnect the SST from the battery.
Precaution. (
Click here
)
Disconnect the cable from the negative battery terminal.
NOTICE:
Wait for 90 seconds after disconnecting the cable to prevent the airbag working.
Remove the steering column cover lower.
While turning the steering wheel assembly to the right and left, remove the 3 screws and steering column cover lower.
NOTICE:
When handling the airbag connector, take care not to damage the airbag wire harness.
Install the SST.
CAUTION:
Check that there is no looseness in the steering wheel assembly and steering pad.
Disconnect the airbag connector (yellow colored one) from the spiral cable with steering sensor.
Connect the SST connector to the airbag connector of the spiral cable with steering sensor.
SST
09082-00700
09082-00760
NOTICE:
To avoid damaging the SST connector and wire harness, do not lock the secondary lock of the twin lock.
Move the SST at least 10 m (33 ft) away from the vehicle front side window.
Maintaining enough clearance for the SST wire harness in the front side window, close all doors and windows of the vehicle.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the SST wire harness.
Connect the red clip of the SST to the battery positive (+) terminal and the black clip of the SST to the negative (-) terminal.
Deploy the airbag.
Check that no one is inside the vehicle or within a 10 m (33 ft) radius of the vehicle.
Press the SST activation switch and deploy the airbag.
CAUTION:
When deploying the airbag, make sure that no one is near the vehicle.
The steering pad becomes extremely hot when the airbag is deployed, so do not touch it for at least 30 minutes after deployment.
Use gloves and safety glasses when handling a steering pad with a deployed airbag.
Do not apply water, etc. to a steering pad with a deployed airbag.
Always wash your hands with water after completing the operation.
HINT:
The airbag is deployed as the LED of the SST activation switch comes on.
2. DISPOSE OF HORN BUTTON ASSEMBLY (WHEN NOT INSTALLED IN VEHICLE)
NOTICE:
When disposing of the steering pad, never use the customer's vehicle to deploy the airbag.
Be sure to follow the procedure detailed below when deploying the airbag.
HINT:
Prepare a battery as the power source to deploy the airbag.
Check the function of the SST.
SST
09082-00700
Remove the steering pad. (
Click here
)
CAUTION:
When removing the steering pad, work must be started 90 seconds after the ignition switch is turned off and the negative (-) terminal cable is disconnected from the battery.
When storing the steering pad, keep the airbag deployment side facing upward.
Using a service-purpose wire harness for the vehicle, tie down the steering pad to the disc wheel.
Wire harness:
Stripped wire harness section
1.25 mm
2
or more (0.0019 in.
2
or more)
CAUTION:
If the wire harness is too thin or an alternative object is used to tie down the steering pad, it may be snapped by the shock when the airbag is deployed. Always use a wire harness for vehicle use with an area of at least 1.25 mm
2
(0.0019 in.
2
).
HINT:
To calculate the area of the stripped wire harness section:
Area = 3.14 x (Diameter)
2
divided by 4
Install the 2 bolts with washers into the 2 bolt holes on the steering pad.
Bolt:
L:
35.0 mm (1.378 in.)
M:
6.0 mm (0.236 in.)
Pitch:
1.0mm (1.378 in.)
NOTICE:
Tighten the bolts by hand until they become difficult to turn.
Do not tighten the bolts excessively.
After connecting the SST below to each other, connect them to the steering pad.
SST
09082-00802
(09082-10801, 09082-40800)
Using 3 wire harnesses, wind wire harness at least 2 times each around the bolts installed on the left and right sides of the steering pad.
CAUTION:
Tightly wind the wire harness around the bolts so that there is no slack.
Make sure that the wire harness is tight. If there is slack in wire harness, the steering pad may become loose due to the shock when the airbag is deployed.
Face the airbag deployment side of the steering pad upward. Separately tie the left and right sides of the steering pad to the disc wheel through the hub nut holes.
Position the SST connector so that it hangs downward through the hub hole of the disc wheel.
CAUTION:
Make sure that the wire harness is tight. If there is slack in wire harness, the steering pad may become loose due to the shock when the airbag is deployed.
Always tie down the steering pad with the airbag deployment side facing upward.
NOTICE:
The disc wheel will be marked by the airbag deployment, so use an extra disc wheel.
Install the SST.
CAUTION:
Place the disc wheel on a level ground.
Connect the SST connector.
SST
09082-00700
NOTICE:
To avoid damaging the SST connector and wire harness, do not lock the secondary lock of the twin lock. Also, secure some slack for the SST wire harness inside the disc wheel.
Move the SST at least 10 m (33 ft) away from the steering pad tied down to the disc wheel.
Cover the steering pad with a cardboard box or tires.
Covering method using a cardboard box:
Cover the steering pad with the cardboard box and place the weights on the cardboard box and place the weights on the cardboard box in 4 places with at least 190 N (19 kg, 43 lb).
Cardboard box size:
Must exceed the following dimensions
X:
460 mm (18.11 in.)
Y:
650 mm (25.59 in.)
NOTICE:
When the dimension Y of the cardboard box exceeds the diameter of the disc wheel with tire which the steering pad is tied to, X should be the following size.
X = 460 mm (18.11 in.) + width of tire
If a cardboard box which is smaller than the specified size is used, the cardboard box will be broken by the shock from the airbag deployment.
Covering method using tires:
Place at least 3 tires without disc wheels on the tire with disc wheel which the steering pad is tied to.
Place the tire with disc wheel on them.
Tire size:
Must exceed the following dimensions
Width:
185 mm (7.28 in.)
Inner diameter:
360 mm (14.17 in.)
CAUTION:
Do not use tires with disc wheels except on the top and bottom.
NOTICE:
The tires may be marked by the airbag deployment, so use an extra tire.
Do not place the SST connector under the tire because it could be damaged.
Tie the tires together with 2 wire harnesses.
CAUTION:
Make sure that the wire harness is tight. Looseness in the wire harness results in the tires coming free due to the shock when the airbag is deployed.
Deploy the airbag.
Connect the red clip of the SST to the battery positive (+) terminal and the black clip of the SST to the battery negative (-) terminal.
Check that no one is within a 10 m (33 ft) radius of the disc wheel which the steering pad is tied to.
Press the SST activation switch and deploy the airbag.
CAUTION:
When deploying the airbag, make sure that no one is near the tire.
HINT:
The airbag is deployed as the LED of the SST activation switch comes on.
Dispose of the steering pad.
CAUTION:
The steering pad becomes extremely hot when the airbag is deployed, so do not touch it for at least 30 minutes after deployment.
Use gloves and safety glasses when handling a steering pad with a deployed airbag.
Do not apply water, etc. to a steering pad with a deployed airbag.
Always wash your hands with water after completing the operation.
Remove the steering pad from the disc wheel.
Place the steering pad in a plastic bag, tie it tightly and dispose of it as other general part disposal.

### STEERING PAD > INSTALLATION

STEERING PAD > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL STEERING PAD
Support the steering pad with one hand.
Connect the connector to the steering pad.
NOTICE:
When handling the airbag connector, take care not to damage the airbag wire harness.
Connect the horn connector.
Confirm that the circumference groove of the "torx" screw fits in the screw case, and place the steering pad onto the steering wheel assembly.
Using a "torx" socket wrench (T30), tighten the 2 "torx" screws.
Torque:
8.8 N*m{
90 kgf*cm
, 78 in.*lbf
}
2. INSTALL STEERING WHEEL COVER LOWER NO.3
Install the steering wheel cover lower No.3.
3. INSTALL STEERING WHEEL COVER LOWER NO.2
Install the steering wheel cover lower No.2.
4. CONNECT CABLE TO BATTERY NEGATIVE TERMINAL
5. INSPECT STEERING PAD
With the steering pad installed on the vehicle, perform a visual check. If there are any defects as mentioned below, replace the steering pad with a new one:
Cuts, minute cracks or marked discoloration on the steering pad top surface or in the grooved portion.
Make sure that the horn sounds.
HINT:
If the horn does not sound, inspect the horn system.
6. INSPECT SUPPLEMENTAL RESTRAINT SYSTEM WARNING LIGHT
(
Click here
)
7. PERFORM INITIALIZATION
Some system need initialization when reconnecting battery cable. (
Click here
)

### STEERING PAD > REMOVAL

STEERING PAD > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. DISCONNECT CABLE FROM BATTERY NEGATIVE TERMINAL
NOTICE:
Wait for 90 seconds after disconnecting the cable to prevent the airbag working.
2. REMOVE STEERING WHEEL COVER LOWER NO.2
Using a screwdriver, remove the steering wheel No.2 cover lower.
HINT:
Tape up the screwdriver tip before use.
3. REMOVE STEERING WHEEL COVER LOWER NO.3
Using a screwdriver, remove the steering wheel No.3 cover lower.
HINT:
Tape up the screwdriver tip before use.
4. REMOVE STEERING PAD
Using a "torx" socket wrench (T30), loosen the 2 "torx" screws until the groove along the screw circumference catches on the screw case.
Pull out the steering pad from the steering wheel assembly and support the steering pad with one hand.
Disconnect the horn connector.
Disconnect the connector and remove the steering pad.
NOTICE:
When handling the airbag connector, take care not to damage the airbag wire harness.

---

### STEERING PAD > ON-VEHICLE INSPECTION

STEERING PAD > ON-VEHICLE INSPECTION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSPECT STEERING PAD (VEHICLE NOT INVOLVED IN COLLISION)
Perform a diagnostic system check. (
Click here
)
With the steering pad installed on the vehicle, perform a visual check. If there are any defects as mentioned below, replace the steering pad with a new one:
Cuts, minute cracks or marked discoloration on the steering pad top surface or in the grooved portion.
2. INSPECT STEERING PAD (VEHICLE INVOLVED IN COLLISION AND AIRBAG HAS NOT DEPLOYED)
Perform a diagnostic system check. (
Click here
)
With the steering pad removed from the vehicle, perform a visual check. If there are any defects as mentioned below, replace the steering pad or steering wheel assembly with a new one:
Cuts, minute cracks or marked discoloration on the steering pad top surface or in the grooved portion.
Cracks or other damage to the connectors.
Deformation of the steering wheel assembly.
Deformation of the horn button contact plate of the steering pad.
There should be no interference between the steering pad and steering wheel assembly, and the clearance should be uniform all the way around when the new steering pad is installed on the steering wheel assembly.
CAUTION:
For removal and installation procedures of the steering pad, be sure to follow the correct procedure.

### BRAKE SYSTEM > PRECAUTION

BRAKE SYSTEM > PRECAUTION
for Preparation
Click here
NOTICE:
Care must be taken to replace each part properly as it could affect the performance of the brake system and result in a driving hazard. Replace the parts with those having the same part number or equivalent.
It is very important to keep parts and the area clean when repairing the brake system.
If the vehicle is equipped with a mobile communication system, refer to the precaution in the INTRODUCTION section.

### BRAKE FLUID > BLEEDING

BRAKE FLUID > BLEEDING
for Preparation
Click here
NOTICE:
Immediately wash off brake fluid that comes into contact with any painted surface.
HINT:
If any work is done on the brake system or if air in the brake lines is suspected, bleed the air from the system.
1. ADD BRAKE FLUID
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No. 116 DOT3 or equivalent
2. BLEED BRAKE MASTER CYLINDER
HINT:
If the master cylinder has been disassembled or if the reservoir becomes empty, bleed the air from the master cylinder.
Using SST, disconnect the brake lines from the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Slowly depress the brake pedal and hold it there.
Block the outer holes with your fingers, and release the brake pedal.
Remove your fingers. Slowly depress the brake pedal and hold it again. Block the outer holes with your fingers and release the brake pedal. Repeat this procedure 3 or 4 times.
Using SST, connect the brake lines to the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
3. BLEED BRAKE LINE
Connect the vinyl tube to the bleeder plug.
Depress the brake pedal several times and loosen the bleeder plug with the pedal held down.
At the point where the fluid stops coming out, tighten the bleeder plug and release the brake pedal.
Repeat this procedure until the air in the brake fluid is completely bled out.
Tighten the bleeder plug.
Front bleeder plug:
Torque:
10.8 N*m{
110 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Rear bleeder plug:
Torque:
11.0 N*m{
112 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Repeat the above procedure to bleed the air out of the brake line for each wheel.
4. CHECK BRAKE FLUID LEVEL IN RESERVOIR
Check the fluid level and add fluid if necessary.
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No.116 DOT3 or equivalent

---

### BRAKE PEDAL > ADJUSTMENT

BRAKE PEDAL > ADJUSTMENT
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSPECT BRAKE PEDAL HEIGHT
Check brake pedal height.
Pedal height from floor:
151 to 161 mm (5.945 to 6.339 in.) for left hand drive
154 to 164 mm (6.063 to 6.457 in.) for right hand drive
2. ADJUST BRAKE PEDAL HEIGHT
Disconnect the stop light switch connector.
Turn the stop light switch assembly counter-clockwise and remove it.
Remove the brake booster (
Click here
).
Loosen the push rod lock nut.
Adjust the pedal height by turning the pedal push rod clevis.
Tighten the push rod lock nut.
Torque:
26 N*m{
260 kgf*cm
, 19 ft.*lbf
}
Install the brake booster (
Click here
).
Insert the stop light switch into the adjuster until it slightly touches the cushion.
CAUTION:
Do not depress the brake pedal.
Turn the stop light switch assembly clockwise by approximately 90°.
CAUTION:
Do not depress the brake pedal.
Connect the stop light switch connector.
Check the clearance of the shaft.
Clearance:
0.5 to 2.6 mm (0.020 to 0.102 in.)
3. INSPECT BRAKE PEDAL FREE PLAY
Stop the engine and depress the brake pedal several times until there is no more vacuum left in the booster.
Push in the pedal until the beginning of the resistance is felt. Measure the distance as shown in the illustration.
Pedal free play:
1.0 to 6.0 mm (0.039 to 0.236 in.)
4. INSPECT BRAKE PEDAL RESERVE DISTANCE
Stop the engine and depress the brake pedal several times until there is no more vacuum left in the booster.
Release the parking brake handle.
With the engine running, depress the pedal and measure the pedal reserve distance as shown in the illustration.
Pedal reserve distance from floor:
More than 121 mm (4.76 in.) at 500 N (51 kgf, 112.5 lbf)
NOTICE:
Remove the floor mat before measuring the distance.
