### BRAKE PEDAL > INSTALLATION

BRAKE PEDAL > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. TEMPORARILY TIGHTEN BRAKE PEDAL SUPPORT ASSEMBLY
Using the 5 bolts and nut, temporarily install the brake pedal support assembly to the dash panel.
2. INSTALL ACCELERATOR PEDAL ROD ASSEMBLY
Install the accelerator pedal rod assembly with the 2 nuts.
Torque:
5.0 N*m{
51 kgf*cm
, 44 in.*lbf
}
3. CONNECT WIRE HARNESS
Connect the 2 connectors.
4. INSTALL BRAKE BOOSTER GASKET
5. INSTALL BRAKE BOOSTER ASSEMBLY
Install the brake booster assembly with the 4 nuts.
Torque:
14 N*m{
145 kgf*cm
, 10 ft.*lbf
}
Connect the vacuum hose with the clip.
Fro 2KD-FTV, 5L-E engine type:
Connect the vacuum warning switch connector to the brake booster assembly.
6. FULLY TIGHTEN BRAKE PEDAL SUPPORT ASSEMBLY
Fully tighten the 5 bolts and nut.
Torque:
31 N*m{
316 kgf*cm
, 23 ft.*lbf
}
Fully tighten the nut.
Torque:
34 N*m{
350 kgf*cm
, 25 ft.*lbf
}
7. INSTALL BRAKE TUBE
Automatic transmission:
Connect the 2 tubes to the clamp.
Manual transmission:
Connect the 3 tubes to the clamp.
Using SST, connect the clutch tube to the clutch master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
8. INSTALL MASTER CYLINDER PUSH ROD CLEVIS
Apply lithium soap base glycol grease to the clevis pin.
Install the push rod clevis to the brake pedal support assembly with the clevis pin and a new clip.
9. INSTALL INSTRUMENT PANEL FINISH PANEL LOWER
Engage the 4 clips (B).
Install the instrument panel finish panel lower with the 2 clips (A).
10. INSTALL HEADLIGHT ASSEMBLY LH (RHD (NARROW BODY TYPE))
Install of the headlight assembly RH is the same as the headlight assembly LH. (
Click here
)
11. INSTALL HEADLIGHT ASSEMBLY RH (RHD (WIDE BODY TYPE))
Install of the headlight assembly RH is the same as the headlight assembly LH. (
Click here
)
12. INSTALL AIR INLET DUCT NO.2 (LHD (W/ INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
13. INSTALL AIR INLET DUCT NO.1 (LHD (W/ INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
14. INSTALL RADIATOR SUPPORT SEAL UPPER (LHD (W/O INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
15. INSTALL FRONT BUMPER SIDE SUPPORT RH (RHD (BODY TYPE WIDE))
16. INSTALL FRONT BUMPER COVER (RHD (BODY TYPE WIDE))
17. INSTALL STEP PLATE COVER RH (RHD (BODY TYPE WIDE))
18. INSTALL STEP PLATE COVER LH (RHD (BODY TYPE WIDE))
19. INSTALL RADIATOR GRILLE (W/O INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY)
20. INSTALL BRAKE MASTER CYLINDER
Install the brake master cylinder and harness clamp bracket to the booster with the 2 nuts.
Torque:
12.7 N*m{
130 kgf*cm
, 9 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Check that the stopper of the harness clamp bracket is securely holding on the brake master cylinder assembly.
Manual Transmission:
Install the clutch reservoir tube with the clip.
Using SST, connect the 2 brake tubes to the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
Connect the level warning light switch connector.
21. ADD BRAKE FLUID
22. BLEED BRAKE MASTER CYLINDER
HINT:
If the master cylinder has been disassembled or if the reservoir becomes empty, bleed the air from the master cylinder.
Using SST, disconnect the brake lines from the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Slowly depress the brake pedal and hold it there.
Block the outer holes with your fingers, and release the brake pedal.
Remove your fingers. Slowly depress the brake pedal and hold it again. Block the outer holes with your fingers and release the brake pedal. Repeat this procedure 3 or 4 times.
Using SST, connect the brake lines to the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
23. BLEED BRAKE LINE
Connect the vinyl tube to the bleeder plug.
Depress the brake pedal several times and loosen the bleeder plug with the pedal held down.
At the point where the fluid stops coming out, tighten the bleeder plug and release the brake pedal.
Repeat this procedure until the air in the brake fluid is completely bled out.
Tighten the bleeder plug.
Front bleeder plug:
Torque:
10.8 N*m{
110 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Rear bleeder plug:
Torque:
11.0 N*m{
112 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Repeat the above procedure to bleed the air out of the brake line for each wheel.
24. INSPECT BRAKE FLUID LEVEL
Check the fluid level and add fluid if necessary.
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No.116 DOT3 or equivalent
25. INSPECT BRAKE FLUID
26. VEHICLE PREPARATION FOR HEADLIGHT AIM ADJUSTMENT
Prepare the vehicle:
Ensure that there is no damage or deformation to the body around the headlights.
Fill the fuel tank.
Make sure that the oil is filled to the specified level.
Make sure that the coolant is filled to the specified level.
Inflate the tires to the appropriate pressure.
Place the spare tire, tools, and jack in their original positions.
Unload the trunk.
Sit a person of average weight (75 kg, 165 lb) in the driver's seat.
27. PREPARATION FOR HEADLIGHT AIMING (USING A SCREEN)
Prepare the vehicle according to the following conditions:
Place the vehicle in a location that is dark enough to clearly observe the cutoff line. The cutoff line is a distinct line, below which light from the headlights can be observed and above which it cannot.
Place the vehicle at a 90° angle to the wall.
Create a 25 m (82 ft) distance between the vehicle (headlight bulb center) and the wall.
Place the vehicle on a level surface.
Bounce the vehicle up and down to settle the suspension.
NOTICE:
A distance of 25 m (82 ft) between the vehicle (headlight bulb center) and the wall is necessary for proper aim adjustment. If unavailable, secure a distance of exactly 3 m (9.84 ft) for check and adjustment. (The target zone will change with the distance so follow the instructions in the illustration.)
Prepare a piece of thick white paper (approximately 2 m (6.6 ft) (height) x 4 m (13.1 ft) (width)) to use as a screen.
Draw a vertical line down the center of the screen (V line).
Set the screen as shown in the illustration.
HINT:
Stand the screen perpendicular to the ground.
Align the V line on the screen with the center of the vehicle.
Draw base lines (H line, V LH, V RH lines) on the screen as shown in the illustration.
HINT:
The base lines differ for "low-beam inspection" and "high-beam inspection".
Make the headlight bulb center marks on the screen. If the center mark cannot be observed on the headlight, use the center of the headlight bulb or the manufacturer's name marked on the headlight as the center mark.
H Line (Headlight height):
Draw a horizontal line across the screen so that it passes through the center marks. The H line should be at the same height as the headlight bulb center marks of the low-beam headlights.
V LH Line, V RH Line (Center mark position of left-hand (LH) and right-hand (RH) headlights):
Draw two vertical lines so that they intersect the H line at each center mark (aligned with the center of the low-beam headlight bulbs).
28. HEADLIGHT AIMING INSPECTION
Cover or disconnect the connector of the headlight on the opposite side to prevent light from the headlight not being inspected from affecting headlight aiming inspection.
NOTICE:
Do not keep the headlight covered for more than 3 minutes. The headlight lens is made of synthetic resin, and may easily melt or be damaged due to heat.
HINT:
When checking the aim of the high-beam, cover the low-beam or disconnect the connector.
Start the engine.
NOTICE:
Engine rpm must be 1,500 or more.
With headlight leveling switch:
Set the headlight leveling switch to 0 (zero).
Turn on the headlight and make sure that the cutoff line falls within the specified area, as shown in the illustration.
HINT:
Alignment distance is 25 m (82 ft):
The cutoff line is 48 mm (1.88 in.) to 698 mm (27.48 in.) below the H line on low-beams (ECE Reg.48).
Alignment distance is 3 m (9.84 ft):
The cutoff line is 6 mm (0.23 in.) to 84 mm (3.3 in.) below the H line on low-beams (ECE Reg.48).
Alignment distance is 25 m (82 ft):
The cutoff line is 249 mm (9.8 in.) below the H line on low-beams.
Alignment distance is 3 m (9.84 ft):
The cutoff line is 30 mm (1.18 in.) below the H line on low-beams.
Since the low-beam light and the high-beam light are a unit, if the aim on one is correct, the other should also be correct. However, check both beams just to make sure.
29. ADJUST HEADLIGHT AIMING
Adjust the aim vertically:
Adjust the headlight aim into the specified range by turning aiming screw A with a screwdriver.
NOTICE:
The final turn of the aiming screw should be made in the clockwise direction. If the screw is tightened excessively, loosen it and then retighten it, so that the final turn of the screw is in the clockwise direction.
HINT:
Perform low-beam aim adjustment.
The headlight aim moves up when turning the aiming screw clockwise, and moves down when turning the aiming screw counterclockwise.
Adjust the aim horizontally:
Adjust the headlight aim into the specified range by turning aiming screw B with a screwdriver.
NOTICE:
The final turn of the aiming screw should be made in the clockwise direction. If the screw is tightened excessively, loosen it and then retighten it, so that the final turn of the screw is in the clockwise direction.
HINT:
Perform low-beam aim adjustment.
30. INSPECT BRAKE PEDAL HEIGHT
Check brake pedal height.
Pedal height from floor:
151 to 161 mm (5.945 to 6.339 in.) for left hand drive
154 to 164 mm (6.063 to 6.457 in.) for right hand drive
31. ADJUST BRAKE PEDAL HEIGHT
Disconnect the stop light switch connector.
Turn the stop light switch assembly counter-clockwise and remove it.
Remove the brake booster (
Click here
).
Loosen the push rod lock nut.
Adjust the pedal height by turning the pedal push rod clevis.
Tighten the push rod lock nut.
Torque:
26 N*m{
260 kgf*cm
, 19 ft.*lbf
}
Install the brake booster (
Click here
).
Insert the stop light switch into the adjuster until it slightly touches the cushion.
CAUTION:
Do not depress the brake pedal.
Turn the stop light switch assembly clockwise by approximately 90°.
CAUTION:
Do not depress the brake pedal.
Connect the stop light switch connector.
Check the clearance of the shaft.
Clearance:
0.5 to 2.6 mm (0.020 to 0.102 in.)
32. INSPECT BRAKE PEDAL FREE PLAY
Stop the engine and depress the brake pedal several times until there is no more vacuum left in the booster.
Push in the pedal until the beginning of the resistance is felt. Measure the distance as shown in the illustration.
Pedal free play:
1.0 to 6.0 mm (0.039 to 0.236 in.)
33. INSPECT BRAKE PEDAL RESERVE DISTANCE
Stop the engine and depress the brake pedal several times until there is no more vacuum left in the booster.
Release the parking brake handle.
With the engine running, depress the pedal and measure the pedal reserve distance as shown in the illustration.
Pedal reserve distance from floor:
More than 121 mm (4.76 in.) at 500 N (51 kgf, 112.5 lbf)
NOTICE:
Remove the floor mat before measuring the distance.

### BRAKE PEDAL > REMOVAL

BRAKE PEDAL > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. DRAIN BRAKE FLUID
2. REMOVE BRAKE MASTER CYLINDER
Using SST, disconnect the 2 brake tubes from the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Disconnect the warning switch connector.
Manual Transmission:
Slide the clip and separate the clutch reservoir tube.
Remove the 2 nuts, and disconnect the bracket.
Remove the brake master cylinder from brake booster.
3. REMOVE RADIATOR GRILLE (W/O INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY)
Remove the 2 clips and 2 screws.
Release the 6 claws and 4 clips and remove the radiator grille.
4. REMOVE STEP PLATE COVER LH (RHD (BODY TYPE WIDE))
Remove the 4 clips.
Release the claw and remove the step plate cover.
5. REMOVE STEP PLATE COVER RH (RHD (BODY TYPE WIDE))
6. REMOVE FRONT BUMPER COVER (RHD (BODY TYPE WIDE))
Remove the 8 clips and 3 bolts.
Release the 12 claws and remove the bumper.
Disconnect the connectors. (w/ fog light)
7. REMOVE FRONT BUMPER SIDE SUPPORT RH (RHD (BODY TYPE WIDE))
Remove of the bumper side support RH is the same as the bumper side support LH. (
Click here
)
8. REMOVE RADIATOR SUPPORT SEAL UPPER (LHD (W/O INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
Remove the 6 clips and radiator support seal upper.
9. REMOVE AIR INLET DUCT NO.1 (LHD (W/ INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
Remove the 3 clips and the air inlet duct No.2.
Remove the 2 bolts, 2 clips and the air inlet duct No.1.
10. REMOVE AIR INLET DUCT NO.2 (LHD (W/ INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
11. REMOVE HEADLIGHT ASSEMBLY RH (RHD (NARROW BODY TYPE))
Remove of the headlight assembly RH is the same as the headlight assembly LH. (
Click here
)
12. REMOVE HEADLIGHT ASSEMBLY RH (RHD (WIDE BODY TYPE))
Remove of the headlight assembly RH is the same as the headlight assembly LH. (
Click here
)
13. REMOVE INSTRUMENT PANEL FINISH PANEL LOWER
Using a clip remover, remove the 2 clips (A).
Disengage the 4 clips (B).
Disconnect the connector.
Push the positions indicated by the arrows in the illustration to separate the fuel lid lock control cable and bonnet (hood) control cable assembly from the panel.
Remove the instrument panel finish lower.
14. SEPARATE MASTER CYLINDER PUSH ROD CLEVIS
Remove the clip and clevis pin and separate the push rod clevis from the brake pedal.
15. SEPARATE BRAKE TUBE
Manual transmission:
Using SST, disconnect the clutch tube from the clutch master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Disengage the 3 tubes from the clamp.
Automatic transmission:
Disengage the 2 tubes from the clamp.
16. REMOVE BRAKE BOOSTER ASSEMBLY
Slide the clip, disconnect the vacuum hose.
For 2KD-FTV, 5L-E engine type:
Disconnect the vacuum warning switch connector from the brake booster.
Remove the 4 nuts and brake booster assembly.
17. REMOVE BRAKE BOOSTER GASKET
18. DISCONNECT WIRE HARNESS
Disconnect the 2 connectors.
19. REMOVE ACCELERATOR PEDAL ROD ASSEMBLY
Remove the 2 nuts and accelerator pedal rod assembly.
20. REMOVE BRAKE PEDAL SUPPORT ASSEMBLY
Loosening the bolt.
CAUTION:
Do not remove the loosened bolt
HINT:
When loosening the nut, keep the bolt from rotating.
Remove the 5 bolts, nut and the brake pedal support assembly.

BRAKE PEDAL > DISASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here

for Preparation

### BRAKE PEDAL > REASSEMBLY

BRAKE PEDAL > REASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL CUSHION
2. INSTALL STOP LIGHT SWITCH ASSEMBLY
Install the adjuster to the brake pedal support assembly.
Turning clockwise, install the stop light switch assembly to the brake pedal support assembly.
HINT:
When adjusting the brake pedal height, also adjust the installing position of the stop light switch assembly.
3. INSTALL BRAKE PEDAL RETURN SPRING
4. INSTALL BRAKE PEDAL PAD
5. INSTALL BREAK AWAY CAPSULE
Install the break away capsule to the pedal support to cowl plate.
Align the matchmarks on the break away capsule and the brake pedal support assembly.
Install the bolt.
Torque:
26 N*m{
260 kgf*cm
, 19 ft.*lbf
}

### BRAKE BOOSTER > INSTALLATION

BRAKE BOOSTER > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL MASTER CYLINDER PUSH ROD CLEVIS
Temporarily install the push rod clevis with the lock nut.
Turn the push rod clevis and adjust length "A" shown in the illustration.
Length "A":
163.6 to 173.6 mm (6.441 in. to 6.835 in.)
Tighten the push rod lock nut.
Torque:
26 N*m{
265 kgf*cm
, 19 ft.*lbf
}
2. INSTALL BRAKE VACUUM CHECK VALVE ASSEMBLY
Install the grommet and check valve assembly.
3. INSTALL VACUUM WARNING SWITCH ASSEMBLY
Clean the vacuum warning switch assembly.
Apply seal packing black to 2 or 3 threads of the bolts.
Seal packing:
Part No.08826-00080, seal packing black or equivalent
Install the warning switch assembly to the brake booster.
Torque:
13.7 N*m{
140 kgf*cm
, 10 ft.*lbf
}
4. INSTALL BRAKE BOOSTER GASKET
Install a new brake booster gasket to the brake booster.
5. INSTALL BRAKE BOOSTER ASSEMBLY
Install the brake booster assembly with the 4 nuts.
Torque:
14 N*m{
145 kgf*cm
, 10 ft.*lbf
}
Connect the vacuum hose with the clip.
Fro 2KD-FTV, 5L-E engine type:
Connect the vacuum warning switch connector to the brake booster assembly.
6. INSTALL BRAKE TUBE
Automatic transmission:
Connect the 2 tubes to the clamp.
Manual transmission:
Connect the 3 tubes to the clamp.
Using SST, connect the clutch tube to the clutch master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
7. INSTALL MASTER CYLINDER PUSH ROD CLEVIS
Apply lithium soap base glycol grease to the clevis pin.
Install the push rod clevis to the brake pedal support assembly with the clevis pin and a new clip.
8. INSTALL INSTRUMENT PANEL FINISH PANEL LOWER
Engage the 4 clips (B).
Install the instrument panel finish panel lower with the 2 clips (A).
9. INSTALL HEADLIGHT ASSEMBLY RH (RHD (NARROW BODY TYPE))
Install of the headlight assembly RH is the same as the headlight assembly LH. (
Click here
)
10. INSTALL HEADLIGHT ASSEMBLY RH (RHD (WIDE BODY TYPE))
Install of the headlight assembly RH is the same as the headlight assembly LH. (
Click here
)
11. INSTALL FRONT BUMPER SIDE SUPPORT LH (RHD (WIDE BODY TYPE))
12. INSTALL FRONT BUMPER COVER (RHD (WIDE BODY TYPE))
13. INSTALL STEP PLATE COVER LH (RHD (WIDE BODY TYPE))
14. INSTALL STEP PLATE COVER RH (RHD (WIDE BODY TYPE))
15. INSTALL WINDSHIELD WASHER JAR ASSEMBLY (for LHD)
16. INSTALL RADIATOR GRILLE
17. INSTALL BRAKE MASTER CYLINDER
Install the brake master cylinder and harness clamp bracket to the booster with the 2 nuts.
Torque:
12.7 N*m{
130 kgf*cm
, 9 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Check that the stopper of the harness clamp bracket is securely holding on the brake master cylinder assembly.
Manual Transmission:
Install the clutch reservoir tube with the clip.
Using SST, connect the 2 brake tubes to the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
Connect the level warning light switch connector.
18. ADD BRAKE FLUID
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No. 116 DOT3 or equivalent
19. BLEED BRAKE MASTER CYLINDER
HINT:
If the master cylinder has been disassembled or if the reservoir becomes empty, bleed the air from the master cylinder.
Using SST, disconnect the brake lines from the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Slowly depress the brake pedal and hold it there.
Block the outer holes with your fingers, and release the brake pedal.
Remove your fingers. Slowly depress the brake pedal and hold it again. Block the outer holes with your fingers and release the brake pedal. Repeat this procedure 3 or 4 times.
Using SST, connect the brake lines to the master cylinder.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
20. BLEED BRAKE LINE
Connect the vinyl tube to the bleeder plug.
Depress the brake pedal several times and loosen the bleeder plug with the pedal held down.
At the point where the fluid stops coming out, tighten the bleeder plug and release the brake pedal.
Repeat this procedure until the air in the brake fluid is completely bled out.
Tighten the bleeder plug.
Front bleeder plug:
Torque:
10.8 N*m{
110 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Rear bleeder plug:
Torque:
11.0 N*m{
112 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Repeat the above procedure to bleed the air out of the brake line for each wheel.
21. INSPECT BRAKE FLUID LEVEL
Check the fluid level and add fluid if necessary.
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No.116 DOT3 or equivalent
22. CHECK BRAKE FLUID LEAKAGE
23. VEHICLE PREPARATION FOR HEADLIGHT AIM ADJUSTMENT
Prepare the vehicle:
Ensure that there is no damage or deformation to the body around the headlights.
Fill the fuel tank.
Make sure that the oil is filled to the specified level.
Make sure that the coolant is filled to the specified level.
Inflate the tires to the appropriate pressure.
Place the spare tire, tools, and jack in their original positions.
Unload the trunk.
Sit a person of average weight (75 kg, 165 lb) in the driver's seat.
24. PREPARATION FOR HEADLIGHT AIMING (USING A SCREEN)
Prepare the vehicle according to the following conditions:
Place the vehicle in a location that is dark enough to clearly observe the cutoff line. The cutoff line is a distinct line, below which light from the headlights can be observed and above which it cannot.
Place the vehicle at a 90° angle to the wall.
Create a 25 m (82 ft) distance between the vehicle (headlight bulb center) and the wall.
Place the vehicle on a level surface.
Bounce the vehicle up and down to settle the suspension.
NOTICE:
A distance of 25 m (82 ft) between the vehicle (headlight bulb center) and the wall is necessary for proper aim adjustment. If unavailable, secure a distance of exactly 3 m (9.84 ft) for check and adjustment. (The target zone will change with the distance so follow the instructions in the illustration.)
Prepare a piece of thick white paper (approximately 2 m (6.6 ft) (height) x 4 m (13.1 ft) (width)) to use as a screen.
Draw a vertical line down the center of the screen (V line).
Set the screen as shown in the illustration.
HINT:
Stand the screen perpendicular to the ground.
Align the V line on the screen with the center of the vehicle.
Draw base lines (H line, V LH, V RH lines) on the screen as shown in the illustration.
HINT:
The base lines differ for "low-beam inspection" and "high-beam inspection".
Make the headlight bulb center marks on the screen. If the center mark cannot be observed on the headlight, use the center of the headlight bulb or the manufacturer's name marked on the headlight as the center mark.
H Line (Headlight height):
Draw a horizontal line across the screen so that it passes through the center marks. The H line should be at the same height as the headlight bulb center marks of the low-beam headlights.
V LH Line, V RH Line (Center mark position of left-hand (LH) and right-hand (RH) headlights):
Draw two vertical lines so that they intersect the H line at each center mark (aligned with the center of the low-beam headlight bulbs).
25. HEADLIGHT AIMING INSPECTION
Cover or disconnect the connector of the headlight on the opposite side to prevent light from the headlight not being inspected from affecting headlight aiming inspection.
NOTICE:
Do not keep the headlight covered for more than 3 minutes. The headlight lens is made of synthetic resin, and may easily melt or be damaged due to heat.
HINT:
When checking the aim of the high-beam, cover the low-beam or disconnect the connector.
Start the engine.
NOTICE:
Engine rpm must be 1,500 or more.
With headlight leveling switch:
Set the headlight leveling switch to 0 (zero).
Turn on the headlight and make sure that the cutoff line falls within the specified area, as shown in the illustration.
HINT:
Alignment distance is 25 m (82 ft):
The cutoff line is 48 mm (1.88 in.) to 698 mm (27.48 in.) below the H line on low-beams (ECE Reg.48).
Alignment distance is 3 m (9.84 ft):
The cutoff line is 6 mm (0.23 in.) to 84 mm (3.3 in.) below the H line on low-beams (ECE Reg.48).
Alignment distance is 25 m (82 ft):
The cutoff line is 249 mm (9.8 in.) below the H line on low-beams.
Alignment distance is 3 m (9.84 ft):
The cutoff line is 30 mm (1.18 in.) below the H line on low-beams.
Since the low-beam light and the high-beam light are a unit, if the aim on one is correct, the other should also be correct. However, check both beams just to make sure.
26. ADJUST HEADLIGHT AIMING
Adjust the aim vertically:
Adjust the headlight aim into the specified range by turning aiming screw A with a screwdriver.
NOTICE:
The final turn of the aiming screw should be made in the clockwise direction. If the screw is tightened excessively, loosen it and then retighten it, so that the final turn of the screw is in the clockwise direction.
HINT:
Perform low-beam aim adjustment.
The headlight aim moves up when turning the aiming screw clockwise, and moves down when turning the aiming screw counterclockwise.
Adjust the aim horizontally:
Adjust the headlight aim into the specified range by turning aiming screw B with a screwdriver.
NOTICE:
The final turn of the aiming screw should be made in the clockwise direction. If the screw is tightened excessively, loosen it and then retighten it, so that the final turn of the screw is in the clockwise direction.
HINT:
Perform low-beam aim adjustment.
27. INSPECT BRAKE PEDAL HEIGHT
Check brake pedal height.
Pedal height from floor:
151 to 161 mm (5.945 to 6.339 in.) for left hand drive
154 to 164 mm (6.063 to 6.457 in.) for right hand drive

### BRAKE BOOSTER > ON-VEHICLE INSPECTION

for Preparation
Click here
1. INSPECT BRAKE BOOSTER
Air tightness check.
Start the engine and stop it after 1 or 2 minutes. Depress the brake pedal several times slowly.
HINT:
If the pedal can be depressed to the floor on the first time, but on the 2nd or 3rd time cannot be depressed as far, the booster is airtight.
Depress the brake pedal while the engine is running, and stop the engine with the pedal depressed.
HINT:
If there is no change in the pedal reserve distance while holding the pedal for 30 seconds, the booster is airtight.
Operating check.
Depress the brake pedal several times with the ignition switch off and check that there is no change in the pedal reserve distance when the pedal is depressed.
Start the engine with the brake pedal depressed.
HINT:
If the pedal goes down slightly, operation is normal.

---

### FRONT BRAKE > INSTALLATION

FRONT BRAKE > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL DISC BRAKE CYLINDER ASSEMBLY
Install the disc brake cylinder assembly with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
34 N*m{
350 kgf*cm
, 25 ft.*lbf
}
2. CONNECT FRONT FLEXIBLE HOSE
Connect the flexible hose with the union bolt and a new gasket.
Torque:
30 N*m{
310 kgf*cm
, 22 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Make sure that the stopper of the flexible hose No.1 is securely holding on the cylinder assembly.
3. ADD BRAKE FLUID
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No. 116 DOT3 or equivalent
4. BLEED BRAKE LINE
Connect the vinyl tube to the bleeder plug.
Depress the brake pedal several times and loosen the bleeder plug with the pedal held down.
At the point where the fluid stops coming out, tighten the bleeder plug and release the brake pedal.
Repeat this procedure until the air in the brake fluid is completely bled out.
Tighten the bleeder plug.
Front bleeder plug:
Torque:
10.8 N*m{
110 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Rear bleeder plug:
Torque:
11.0 N*m{
112 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Repeat the above procedure to bleed the air out of the brake line for each wheel.
5. INSPECT BRAKE FLUID LEVEL
Check the fluid level and add fluid if necessary.
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No.116 DOT3 or equivalent
6. CHECK BRAKE FLUID LEAKAGE
7. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}

---

### FRONT BRAKE > INSPECTION

FRONT BRAKE > INSPECTION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSPECT BRAKE CYLINDER AND PISTON
Check the cylinder bore and piston for rust or scoring.
2. INSPECT FRONT DISC BRAKE PAD THICKNESS
Using a ruler, measure the pad lining thickness.
Standard thickness:
10.0 mm (0.390 in.)
Minimum thickness:
1.0 mm (0.039 in.)
3. INSPECT FRONT DISC BRAKE PAD SUPPORT PLATE NO.1
Make sure that the pad support plate No.1 has sufficient rebound, has no deformation, cracks or wear, and that all rust and dirt are cleaned off. If necessary, replace the brake pad support plate No.1.
4. INSPECT FRONT DISC BRAKE PAD SUPPORT PLATE NO.2
Make sure that the pad support plate No.2 has sufficient rebound, has no deformation, cracks or wear, and that all rust and dirt are cleaned off. If necessary, replace the brake pad support plate No.2.
5. INSPECT BRAKE DISC THICKNESS
Using a micrometer, measure the disc thickness.
Standard thickness:
28.0 mm (1.102 in.)
Minimum thickness:
26.0 mm (1.024 in.)

---

### FRONT BRAKE > REMOVAL

FRONT BRAKE > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL
2. DRAIN BRAKE FLUID
3. DISCONNECT FRONT FLEXIBLE HOSE
Remove the union bolt and gasket from the disc brake cylinder assembly, and disconnect the flexible hose.
4. REMOVE DISC BRAKE CYLINDER ASSEMBLY
Remove the 2 bolts and disc brake cylinder assembly.

---

### FRONT BRAKE > DISASSEMBLY

FRONT BRAKE > DISASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE PAD KIT
Remove the 2 brake pads with anti-squeal shims.
2. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE ANTI SQUEAL SHIM KIT
Remove the 4 anti-squeal shims from the brake pad.
Remove the pad wear indicator from each pad.
3. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE PAD SUPPORT PLATE
4. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE PAD SUPPORT PLATE
5. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER SLIDE PIN
Remove the cylinder slide pin from the cylinder mounting.
6. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER SLIDE BUSH
Using a screwdriver, remove the cylinder slide bush from the cylinder slide pin.
HINT:
Tape the screwdriver tip before use.
7. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER SLIDE PIN NO.2
Remove the cylinder slide pin No.2 from the cylinder mounting.
8. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER MOUNTING
Remove the 2 bolts and cylinder mounting.
9. REMOVE BUSH DUST BOOT NO.1
Secure the cylinder mounting in a vise.
Using a screwdriver and a plastic hammer, remove the 2 bush dust boots.
10. REMOVE CYLINDER BOOT
Using a screwdriver, remove the set ring.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the inner cylinder or cylinder groove.
HINT:
Tape the screwdriver tip before use.
Remove the cylinder boot.
11. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE PISTON
Place a shop rag or a piece of cloth between the piston and the disc brake cylinder assembly.
Use compressed air to remove the piston from the disc brake cylinder assembly.
CAUTION:
Do not place your fingers in front of the piston when using compressed air.
NOTICE:
Do not spatter the brake fluid.
12. REMOVE PISTON SEAL
Using a screwdriver, remove the piston seal from the disc brake cylinder assembly.
HINT:
Tape the screwdriver tip before use.
13. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE BLEEDER PLUG CAP
14. REMOVE FRONT DISC BRAKE BLEEDER PLUG
15. SEPARATE FRONT SPEED SENSOR
Remove the 2 bolts, and separate the speed sensor from the steering knuckle.
NOTICE:
Be careful not to damage the speed sensor.
Prevent foreign matter from adhering to the speed sensor.
16. SEPARATE TIE ROD END SUB-ASSEMBLY
Remove the cotter pin and castle nut.
Using SST, separate the tie rod end from the steering knuckle.
SST
09628-00011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Do not damage the brake dust cover.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the vehicle.
17. REMOVE FRONT AXLE ASSEMBLY
Remove the 4 bolts, 4 nuts and front axle assembly from the front suspension lower arm.
Remove the cotter pin and castle nut.
Using SST, remove the front axle assembly from the front suspension upper arm.
SST
09628-62011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Apply grease to the threads and tip of the SST center bolt before use.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the vehicle.
18. REMOVE FRONT LOWER BALL JOINT ASSEMBLY
Fix the steering knuckle in the vise using aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the steering knuckle.
Remove the cotter pin.
Loosen the nut.
Using SST, separate the front lower ball joint assembly from the steering knuckle.
SST
09628-00011
NOTICE:
Do not damage the ball joint dust boot.
Make sure that the string of the SST is securely tied to the steering knuckle.
Remove the nut and front lower ball joint assembly.
19. REMOVE INNER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using a chisel, make a hole in the center of the outer knuckle grease retainer cap to insert a brass bar.
Using a brass bar, remove the inner knuckle grease retainer cap from the steering knuckle as shown in the illustration.
20. REMOVE OUTER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, remove the outer knuckle grease retainer cap.
SST
09950-60010
(09951-00300)
09950-70010
(09951-07200)
21. REMOVE STEERING KNUCKLE
Remove the 4 bolts.
Using SST, remove the steering knuckle, brake dust cover from the front axle hub sub-assembly.
SST
09950-40011
(09951-04020, 09952-04010, 09953-04010, 09955-04051)
09950-60010
(09951-00470)
NOTICE:
Apply grease to the threads and tip of the SST center bolt (09953-04010) before use.
22. REMOVE FRONT DISC
Remove the 6 bolts.
Remove the front disc as shown in the illustration.

---

### FRONT BRAKE > REASSEMBLY

FRONT BRAKE > REASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL FRONT DISC
Install the front disc as shown in the illustration.
Install the front disc with the 6 bolts.
Torque:
62 N*m{
632 kgf*cm
, 46 ft.*lbf
}
2. INSTALL INNER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, place the steering knuckle on a press as shown in the illustration.
SST
09527-30010
Temporarily install a new inner knuckle grease retainer cap to the steering knuckle as shown in the illustration.
NOTICE:
Align the center of the cutout in the inner knuckle grease retainer cap and the groove of the steering knuckle.
Using SST and the press, press the inner knuckle grease retainer cap into the steering knuckle until the distance (A) in the illustration meets the standard value.
SST
09950-70010
(09951-07100)
09951-00900
Distance (A):
26 +/- 0.5 mm (1.02 +/- 0.0196 in.)
NOTICE:
Using a vernier caliper, measure the distance (A) at the 3 points indicated by the arrows in the illustration while pressing.
3. INSTALL FRONT LOWER BALL JOINT ASSEMBLY
Fix the steering knuckle in the vise using aluminium plates.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the steering knuckle.
Install the front lower ball joint assembly to the steering knuckle with the nut.
Torque:
170 N*m{
1,730 kgf*cm
, 125 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Ensure that the thread and taper are free of oil etc.
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the front lower ball joint assembly.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
4. INSTALL STEERING KNUCKLE
Install the steering knuckle and brake dust cover to the front axle hub sub-assembly with the 4 bolts.
Torque:
88 N*m{
897 kgf*cm
, 65 ft.*lbf
}
5. INSTALL OUTER KNUCKLE GREASE RETAINER CAP (w/ Cap)
Using SST, install a new outer knuckle grease retainer cap.
SST
09950-60010
(09951-00420)
09950-70010
(09951-07150)
6. INSTALL FRONT AXLE ASSEMBLY
Temporarily install the front axle assembly to the front suspension upper arm and front suspension lower arm.
Fully tighten front suspension lower arm with the 4 bolts and 4 nuts.
Torque:
Nut (A):
90 N*m{
918 kgf*cm
, 66 ft.*lbf
}
Nut (B):
52 N*m{
530 kgf*cm
, 38 ft.*lbf
}
Fully tighten the front suspension upper arm with the nut.
Torque:
113 N*m{
1,147 kgf*cm
, 83 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the front suspension upper arm.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the castle nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
7. INSTALL TIE ROD END SUB-ASSEMBLY
Install the tie rod end sub-assembly to the steering knuckle with the castle nut.
Torque:
50 N*m{
510 kgf*cm
, 37 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not damage the lower ball joint dust boot.
Install a new cotter pin to the tie rod end sub-assembly.
NOTICE:
Further tighten the castle nut up to 60° if the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned.
8. INSTALL FRONT SPEED SENSOR
Install the speed sensor to the steering knuckle with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
8.0 N*m{
82 kgf*cm
, 71 in.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Prevent foreign matter from adhering to the speed sensor.
Be careful not to damage the speed sensor.
Do not twist the sensor wire when installing the speed sensor.
9. INSPECT DISC RUNOUT
Using a dial indicator, measure the disc runout 10 mm (0.39 in.) away from the outer edge of the disc.
Maximum disc runout:
0.07 mm (0.0028 in.)
If the runout exceeds the maximum value, change the installation positions of the disc and axle so that the runout will become minimal. If the runout still exceeds the maximum, check the bearing play in the axial direction and the axle hub runout (
Click here
). If the bearing play and the axle hub runout are normal and that the disc thickness is not within the specified range, grind the disc. If the disc thickness is less than the minimum, replace the disc.
10. TEMPORARILY TIGHTEN FRONT DISC BRAKE BLEEDER PLUG
11. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE BLEEDER PLUG CAP
12. INSTALL PISTON SEAL
Apply lithium soap base glycol grease to a new piston seal.
Install the piston seal to the disc brake cylinder assembly.
NOTICE:
Make sure that the piston seal is securely installed.
13. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE PISTON
Apply lithium soap base glycol grease to the piston and a new cylinder boot.
Install the cylinder boot to the piston.
NOTICE:
Make sure that the cylinder boot is securely installed in the piston groove.
Install the piston to the disc brake cylinder assembly.
NOTICE:
Do not install the piston forcibly in the cylinder assembly.
14. INSTALL CYLINDER BOOT
Install the cylinder boot to the disc brake cylinder assembly.
NOTICE:
Make sure that the cylinder boot is securely installed in the brake cylinder groove.
Using a screwdriver, install a new set ring.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the cylinder boot.
HINT:
Tape the screwdriver tip before use.
15. INSTALL BUSH DUST BOOT NO.1
Secure the cylinder mounting in a vise.
Apply lithium soap base glycol grease to the seal surface of 2 new bush dust boots.
Using a socket wrench (21 mm) and a hammer, install the 2 bush dust boots.
16. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER MOUNTING
Install the cylinder mounting with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
123 N*m{
1,250 kgf*cm
, 91 ft.*lbf
}
17. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER SLIDE PIN NO.2
Apply lithium soap base glycol grease to the sliding part and the seal surface of the cylinder slide pin No.2.
Install the cylinder slide pin No.2 to the cylinder mounting.
18. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER SLIDE BUSH
Apply lithium soap base glycol grease to the contact surface of the new cylinder slide bush and the cylinder slide pin.
Install the cylinder slide bush to the cylinder slide pin.
19. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER SLIDE PIN
Install the cylinder slide pin to the cylinder mounting.
20. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE CYLINDER MOUNTING
21. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE ANTI-SQUEAL SHIM KIT
Apply disc brake grease to the 2 anti-squeal shims No.1 and install them to each pad.
Install the 3 anti-squeal shims to each pad.
Install the 2 pad wear indicator plates to the pads.
22. INSTALL FRONT DISC BRAKE PAD KIT
Install the 2 pads with pad wear indicator plates to the cylinder mounting.

### FRONT BRAKE > ON-VEHICLE INSPECTION

FRONT BRAKE > ON-VEHICLE INSPECTION
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL
2. INSPECT FRONT DISC BRAKE PAD THICKNESS
Check the pad thickness and replace pads if not within specification.
Minimum thickness:
1.0 mm (0.039 in.)
3. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}

### REAR BRAKE > INSTALLATION

REAR BRAKE > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. TEMPORARILY TIGHTEN REAR DRUM BRAKE BLEEDER PLUG
2. INSTALL REAR WHEEL BRAKE CYLINDER CUP KIT
Apply the lithium soap base glycol grease to 2 new wheel cylinder cups and 2 pistons.
Install the 2 wheel cylinder cups on each piston.
Install the compression spring and the 2 pistons to the wheel brake cylinder assembly.
Install the 2 new cylinder dust boots to the wheel brake cylinder assembly.
3. INSTALL REAR WHEEL BRAKE CYLINDER ASSEMBLY
Install the wheel brake cylinder assembly with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
9.5 N*m{
97 kgf*cm
, 84 in.*lbf
}
Using SST, connect the brake tube.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
4. APPLY HIGH TEMPERATURE GREASE
Apply high temperature grease to the shoe attached surface of the backing plate.
5. INSTALL PARKING BRAKE SHOE STRUT SET
Apply the high temperature grease to the adjusting bolt.
Install the tension spring to the show strut set.
6. INSTALL REAR BRAKE SHOE
Using pliers, install the rear brake shoe, shoe strut set, rear cushion lever and shoe lever with a new C-washer.
Connect the parking brake cable No.3 to the front brake shoe.
Using SST, install the rear brake shoe, pin, shoe hold down spring and shoe hold down cup.
SST
09718-00010
7. INSTALL FRONT BRAKE SHOE
Install the return spring to each brake shoe.
Using SST, install the front brake shoe, pin, shoe hold down spring and shoe hold down cup.
SST
09718-00010
Install the brake shoe lower strut.
NOTICE:
Make sure that the shoe lower strut and brake rear cushion lever are securely engaged.
Using needle-nose pliers, install the tension spring.
8. CONNECT PARKING BRAKE SHOE STRUT SET
Using SST, install the upper side tension spring.
SST
09703-30011
9. INSTALL AUTOMATIC ADJUST LEVER
Install the automatic adjust lever and tension spring.
NOTICE:
Make sure to install the automatic adjust lever between the groove of the shoe strut set and brake shoe.
10. INSPECT REAR DRUM BRAKE
Check the each part is installed properly.
NOTICE:
There should be no oil or grease adhering to the friction surfaces of the shoe lining and drum.
If they are not installed correctly, reinstall them as shown in the illustration.
11. INSTALL REAR AXLE BRAKE DRUM GASKET
Install the new brake drum gasket to the rear axle shaft.
12. INSTALL REAR BRAKE DRUM SUB-ASSEMBLY
13. ADJUST REAR DRUM BRAKE SHOE CLEARANCE
Using a screwdriver from a service hole, turn the adjuster to expand the shoes so that the brake shoes touch the brake drum.
Using another screwdriver, push up the automatic adjust lever and turn the adjuster to contract the shoes so that the brake shoe does not touch the brake drum. Then turn the adjuster another 180 degrees to further contract the shoes.
Install the hole plug.
14. ADD BRAKE FLUID
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No. 116 DOT3 or equivalent
15. BLEED BRAKE LINE
Connect the vinyl tube to the bleeder plug.
Depress the brake pedal several times and loosen the bleeder plug with the pedal held down.
At the point where the fluid stops coming out, tighten the bleeder plug and release the brake pedal.
Repeat this procedure until the air in the brake fluid is completely bled out.
Tighten the bleeder plug.
Front bleeder plug:
Torque:
10.8 N*m{
110 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Rear bleeder plug:
Torque:
11.0 N*m{
112 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Repeat the above procedure to bleed the air out of the brake line for each wheel.
16. INSPECT BRAKE FLUID LEVEL
Check the fluid level and add fluid if necessary.
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No.116 DOT3 or equivalent
17. INSTALL REAR WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}
18. INSPECT PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE TRAVEL
Pull firmly on the parking brake control handle.
Release the parking brake lock, and return the parking brake control handle to its off position.
Slowly pull the parking brake control handle all the way up, and count the number of clicks.
Parking brake control handle travel:
10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
HINT:
If the number of clicks falls outside of the specified range, release the parking brake lock and adjust it.
If there are fewer clicks than the specified value, loosen the parking brake wire adjustment nut an the lock nut. With the parking brake cable loosened, adjust the clearance between the brake drum and lining.
19. ADJUST PARKING BRAKE CONTROL HANDLE TRAVEL
Lift the vehicle
Depress the parking brake handle. Hold the parking brake adjusting nut using a wrench, and loosen the lock nut.
Turn the parking brake adjusting nut until the parking brake handle travel meets the above specification.
Parking brake control handle travel:
10 to 16 clicks at 200 N (20 kgf, 44 lbf)
Count the number of clicks after depressing and releasing the parking brake control handle 3 or 4 times.
Check whether the parking brake drags or not.
When operating the parking brake control handle, check that the parking brake indicator light comes on.
Standard:
Brake warning light always comes on at the first click.
Hold the parking brake adjusting nut using a wrench, and tighten the lock nut.
Torque:
14.5 N*m{
148 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
Lower the vehicle.

---

### REAR BRAKE > INSPECTION

REAR BRAKE > INSPECTION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSPECT BRAKE DRUM INSIDE DIAMETER
Using a brake drum gauge or equivalent, measure the inside diameter of the drum.
Standard inside diameter:
254 mm (10.000 in.)
Maximum inside diameter:
256 mm (10.079 in.)
HINT:
If the inside diameter of the brake drum is more than the maximum, replace the brake drum.
2. INSPECT BRAKE SHOES LINING THICKNESS
Using a ruler, measure the thickness of the shoe lining.
Standard thickness:
5.1 mm (0.201 in.)
Minimum thickness:
1.0 mm (0.039 in.)
HINT:
If the lining thickness is less than the minimum thickness, or if there is severe, uneven wear, replace the brake shoe.
3. INSPECT BRAKE DRUM AND BRAKE SHOE LINING FOR PROPER CONTACT
Apply chalk to the inside surface of the drum, then grind drum on the brake shoe lining to fit.
HINT:
If the contact between the drum and the shoe lining is improper, repair it using a brake shoe grinder or replace the brake shoe assembly.
4. INSPECT BRAKE WHEEL CYLINDER
Check the cylinder bore and the piston for rust or scoring.
HINT:
If necessary, replace the wheel brake cylinder assembly.

---

### REAR BRAKE > REMOVAL

REAR BRAKE > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE REAR WHEEL
2. DRAIN BRAKE FLUID
3. REMOVE REAR BRAKE DRUM SUB-ASSEMBLY
Release the parking brake lever, and remove the brake drum.
HINT:
If the brake drum cannot be removed easily, perform the following steps.
Remove the hole plug and insert a screwdriver through the hole in the backing plate, and hold the automatic adjusting lever away from the adjuster.
Using another screwdriver, contract the brake shoe adjuster by turning the adjusting wheel.
4. REMOVE REAR AXLE BRAKE DRUM GASKET
5. REMOVE AUTOMATIC ADJUST LEVER
Remove the automatic adjust lever and the tension spring.
6. SEPARATE PARKING BRAKE SHOE STRUT SET
Using SST, remove the upper side tension spring from the brake shoe.
SST
09703-30011
7. REMOVE FRONT BRAKE SHOE
Using a needle-nose pliers, remove the anchor side tension spring.
Using SST, remove the cup, shoe hold-down sprig and  pin.
SST
09718-00010
Remove the parking brake shoe lower strut.
Separate the brake shoe return spring, and remove the front brake shoe.
8. REMOVE REAR BRAKE SHOE
Using SST, remove the cup, shoe hold-down sprig and pin.
SST
09718-00010
Disconnect the parking brake cable No.3 and remove the rear brake shoe.
9. REMOVE PARKING BRAKE SHOE STRUT SET
Using a screwdriver, remove the C-washer, brake shoe lever, rear cushion lever and brake shoe strut set from the brake shoe.
Remove the adjusting bolt and tension spring from the brake shoe strut set.
10. REMOVE REAR WHEEL BRAKE CYLINDER ASSEMBLY
Using SST, disconnect the brake tube, use a container to catch brake fluid.
SST
09023-00101
Remove the 2 bolts and wheel cylinder.
11. REMOVE REAR WHEEL BRAKE CYLINDER CUP KIT
Remove the 2 cylinder dust boots from the wheel cylinder.
Remove the 2 pistons.
Remove the 2 cylinder cups from each piston.
Remove the compression spring.
12. REMOVE REAR DRUM BRAKE BLEEDER PLUG

### BRAKE ACTUATOR > INSTALLATION

BRAKE ACTUATOR > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL BRAKE ACTUATOR ASSEMBLY
Install the brake actuator assembly to the actuator bracket with the 2 nuts.
Torque:
5.4 N*m{
55 kgf*cm
, 48 in.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not remove the hole plug until the new actuator assembly is connected because it already contains brake fluid.
Insert the protrusion of the actuator bolt holder between the positioning claws of the actuator bracket No.1.
Insert the positioning pin of the actuator bracket No.1 into the cushion of the actuator assembly.
2. INSTALL BRAKE ACTUATOR COVER
Install the actuator cover to the brake actuator assembly with the 2 nuts.
Torque:
5.4 N*m{
55 kgf*cm
, 48 in.*lbf
}
3. INSTALL BRAKE TUBE
Using SST, install the 5 brake tubes and brake way No.1 to the brake actuator assembly.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
4. INSTALL BRAKE ACTUATOR ASSEMBLY WITH FRONT BRAKE TUBE WAY NO.1
Install the brake actuator assembly with front brake tube way No.1 and the brake tube to the body with the 5 bolts.
Torque:
20 N*m{
204 kgf*cm
, 15 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not bend or damage the brake tube.
Using SST, connect the 6 brake tubes to the front brake tube way No.1.
SST
09023-00101
Torque:
15 N*m{
155 kgf*cm
, 11 ft.*lbf
}
Connect the connector and lock the lock lever.
NOTICE:
Check that the lock lever is securely locked.
5. INSTALL FRONT MUDGUARD
Install the front mudguard with the 3 clips.
6. INSTALL SUB RADIATOR ASSEMBLY
For 2KD-FTV (
Click here
)
For 5L-E (
Click here
)
7. INSTALL HIGH PITCHED HORN ASSEMBLY
8. INSTALL WINDSHIELD WASHER MOTOR AND PUMP ASSEMBLY
Connect the connector and hose.
Install the wind washer jar assembly with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
4.9 N*m{
50 kgf*cm
, 43 in.*lbf
}
9. INSTALL RADIATOR RESERVE TANK ASSEMBLY
Connect the water by-pass hose No.2 with the clip.
Connect the water by-pass hose No.1 with the clip.
Place the reserve tank in the 2 grommets of the radiator upper support, and install the radiator reserve tank assembly with the bolt.
Torque:
7.0 N*m{
71 kgf*cm
, 62 in.*lbf
}
10. INSTALL RADIATOR SUPPORT UPPER
Install the radiator upper support with the 6 bolts and 2 screws.
Torque:
5.5 N*m{
56 kgf*cm
, 49 in.*lbf
}
HINT:
If the position of the radiator upper support goes out of alignment while installing it, remove the front bumper and adjust the hood lock brace and bumper stay.
11. INSTALL HOOD LOCK ASSEMBLY
Connect the hood lock cable assembly to the hood lock assembly.
Temporarily tighten the hood lock assembly with the 3 bolts from the radiator upper support.
Check the hood position and adjust the hood lock assembly position.
Tighten the hood lock assembly with the 3 bolts.
Torque:
12 N*m{
122 kgf*cm
, 9 ft.*lbf
}
12. INSTALL FRONT BUMPER REINFORCEMENT
Install the front bumper reinforcement with the 6 bolts.
Torque:
61 N*m{
622 kgf*cm
, 45 ft.*lbf
}
13. INSTALL HOOD LOCK SUPPORT BRACE SUB-ASSEMBLY
14. INSTALL AIR INLET DUCT NO.1 (LHD (W/ INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
15. INSTALL AIR INLET DUCT NO.2 (LHD (W/ INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
16. INSTALL RADIATOR SUPPORT SEAL UPPER (LHD (W/O INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
Install the air inlet duct No.1 with the 6 clamps.
17. INSTALL FRONT BUMPER
18. INSTALL STEP PLATE COVER RH
19. INSTALL STEP PLATE COVER LH
20. INSTALL RADIATOR GRILLE
21. INSTALL ENGINE UNDER COVER NO.1
22. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}
23. ADD BRAKE FLUID
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No. 116 DOT3 or equivalent
24. BLEED BRAKE LINE
Connect the vinyl tube to the bleeder plug.
Depress the brake pedal several times and loosen the bleeder plug with the pedal held down.
At the point where the fluid stops coming out, tighten the bleeder plug and release the brake pedal.
Repeat this procedure until the air in the brake fluid is completely bled out.
Tighten the bleeder plug.
Front bleeder plug:
Torque:
10.8 N*m{
110 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Rear bleeder plug:
Torque:
11.0 N*m{
112 kgf*cm
, 8 ft.*lbf
}
Repeat the above procedure to bleed the air out of the brake line for each wheel.
25. INSPECT BRAKE FLUID LEVEL
Check the fluid level and add fluid if necessary.
Fluid:
SAE J1704 or FMVSS No.116 DOT3 or equivalent
26. ADD ENGINE COOLANT
For 2TR-FE (
Click here
)
For 2KD-FTV (
Click here
)
For 5L-E (
Click here
)

### BRAKE ACTUATOR > REMOVAL

BRAKE ACTUATOR > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. DRAIN BRAKE FLUID
2. DRAIN ENGINE COOLANT
For 2TR-FE (
Click here
)
For 2KD-FTV (
Click here
)
For 5L-E (
Click here
)
3. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL
4. REMOVE ENGINE UNDER COVER NO.1
5. REMOVE RADIATOR GRILLE
Remove the 2 clips and 2 screws.
Release the 6 claws and 4 clips and remove the radiator grille.
6. REMOVE RADIATOR SUPPORT SEAL UPPER (LHD (W/O INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
Remove the 6 clips and radiator support seal upper.
7. REMOVE AIR INLET DUCT NO.1 (LHD (W/ INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
Remove the 3 clips and the air inlet duct No.2.
Remove the 2 bolts, 2 clips and the air inlet duct No.1.
8. REMOVE AIR INLET DUCT NO.2 (LHD (W/ INTERCOOLER ASSEMBLY))
9. REMOVE STEP PLATE COVER LH
Remove the 4 clips.
Release the claw and remove the step plate cover.
10. REMOVE STEP PLATE COVER RH
11. REMOVE FRONT BUMPER
Remove the 8 clips and 3 bolts.
Release the 12 claws and remove the bumper.
Disconnect the connectors. (w/ fog light)
12. REMOVE HOOD LOCK SUPPORT BRACE SUB-ASSEMBLY
13. REMOVE FRONT BUMPER REINFORCEMENT
Remove the 6 bolts and front bumper reinforcement.
14. REMOVE HOOD LOCK ASSEMBLY
Remove the 3 bolts, hood lock control cable and the hood lock assembly.
15. REMOVE RADIATOR SUPPORT UPPER
Remove the 6 bolts, 2 screws and the radiator upper support.
16. REMOVE RADIATOR RESERVE TANK ASSEMBLY
Remove the bolt, and separate the radiator reserve tank assembly from the body panel.
Remove the clip, and disconnect the water by-pass hose No.1.
Remove the clip, and disconnect the water by-pass hose No.2.
Remove the radiator reserve tank assembly.
17. REMOVE WINDSHIELD WASHER MOTOR AND PUMP ASSEMBLY
Remove the 2 bolts.
Separate the hose and connector, and remove the windshield washer jar assembly.
18. REMOVE HIGH PITCHED HORN ASSEMBLY
19. REMOVE SUB RADIATOR ASSEMBLY
For 2KD-FTV (
Click here
)
For 5L-E (
Click here
)
20. REMOVE FRONT MUDGUARD
Remove the 3 clips and the front mudguard.
21. REMOVE BRAKE ACTUATOR ASSEMBLY WITH FRONT BRAKE TUBE WAY NO.1
Release the lock lever and disconnect the actuator connector.
Using SST, disconnect the 6 brake tubes from the brake tube way No.1.
SST
09023-00101
Remove the 5 bolts. Remove the brake actuator assembly together with the front brake tube way No.1 and brake tube.
NOTICE:
Do not bend or damage the brake tube.
HINT:
Remove these in the direction of the front of the vehicle.
22. REMOVE BRAKE TUBE
Using SST, remove the 5 brake tubes and the brake tube way No.1.
SST
09023-00101
23. REMOVE ACTUATOR BOLT COVER
Remove the 2 nuts and the actuator cover.
24. REMOVE BRAKE ACTUATOR ASSEMBLY
Remove the the 2 nuts and brake actuator assembly from the brake actuator bracket.

---

### BRAKE ACTUATOR > REMOVAL

BRAKE ACTUATOR > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. DRAIN BRAKE FLUID
2. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL
3. REMOVE FRONT BUMPER
for Standard Body:
(
Click here
)
for Wide Body:
(
Click here
)
4. REMOVE FRONT MUDGUARD
Remove the 3 clips and the front mudguard.
5. REMOVE BRAKE ACTUATOR ASSEMBLY WITH FRONT BRAKE TUBE WAY NO. 1
Release the lock lever and disconnect the actuator connector.
Using a union nut wrench, disconnect the 6 brake tubes from the brake tube way No. 1.
Remove the 5 bolts. Remove the brake actuator assembly together with the front brake tube way No. 1 and brake tube.
NOTICE:
Do not bend or damage the brake tube.
HINT:
Remove these in the direction of the front of the vehicle.
6. REMOVE BRAKE TUBE
Using a union nut wrench, remove the 5 brake tubes and the brake tube way No. 1.
7. REMOVE ACTUATOR BOLT COVER
Remove the 2 nuts and the actuator cover.
8. REMOVE BRAKE ACTUATOR ASSEMBLY
Remove the the 2 nuts and brake actuator assembly from the brake actuator bracket.

---

### BRAKE ACTUATOR > DISASSEMBLY

BRAKE ACTUATOR > DISASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE BRAKE ACTUATOR BOLT HOLDER
Remove the 2 actuator bolt holders from the brake actuator assembly.
2. REMOVE BRAKE ACTUATOR BOLT CUSHION
Remove the 3 actuator bolt cushions from the brake actuator assembly.

---

### BRAKE ACTUATOR > REASSEMBLY

BRAKE ACTUATOR > REASSEMBLY
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL BRAKE ACTUATOR BOLT CUSHION
Install the 3 actuator bolt cushions to the brake actuator assembly.
2. INSTALL BRAKE ACTUATOR BOLT HOLDER
Install the 2 actuator bolt holders to the brake actuator assembly.

Measuring points:
A:
Ground clearance of front suspension No.1 adjust cam bolt head
B:
Ground clearance of front center position of rear leaf spring front installation bolt head
NOTICE:
Before inspecting the wheel alignment, adjust the vehicle height to the specified valve.
3. INSPECT TOE-IN
Bounce the vehicle at the corners up and down to stabilize the suspension and inspect the vehicle height.
Inspect toe-in.
Toe-in:
A-B
Toe-in
(total)
2 +- 2 mm (0.08 +- 0.08 in.)
HINT:
If toe-in is not within the specified range, adjust it at the rack ends.
4. ADJUST TOE-IN
Measure the thread lengths of the right and left rack ends.
Standard:
Difference in thread length of 3.0 mm (0.12 in.) or less
Remove the rack boot set clips.
Loosen the tie rod end lock nuts.
Adjust the rack ends if the difference in thread length between the right and left rack ends is not within the specified range.
Extend the longer rack end if the measured toe-in deviates toward the outer-side.
Shorten the shorter rack end if the measured toe-in deviates toward the inner-side.
Turn the right and left rack ends by an equal amount to adjust toe-in.
HINT:
Try to adjust toe-in to the center of the specified range.
Make sure that the lengths of the right and left rack ends are the same.
Standard:
2 +- 1 mm (0.08 +- 0.04 in.)
Tighten the tie rod end lock nuts.
Torque:
74 N*m{
755 kgf*cm
, 55 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Temporarily tighten the lock nut while holding the hexagonal part of the steering rack end so that the lock nut and the steering rack end do not turn together. Hold the width across flat of the tie rod end and tighten the lock nut.
Place the boots on the seats and install the clips.
HINT:
Make sure that the boots are not twisted.
5. INSPECT WHEEL ANGLE
Turn the steering wheel fully left and right and measure the turning angle.
Wheel turning angle:
Wheel angle
Inside wheel
38°00' + 1°30', -2°30'
(38.00° + 1.5°, -2.5°)
Outside wheel:
Reference
33°00' + 1°30', -2°30'
(33.00° + 1.5°, -2.5°)
If the right and left inside wheel angles differ from the specified range, check the right and left rack end lengths.
6. INSPECT CAMBER, CASTER AND STEERING AXIS INCLINATION
Put the front wheel on the center of the alignment tester.
Remove the center ornament.
Install the camber-caster-steering axis inclination gauge at the center of the axle hub or drive shaft.
Inspect the camber, caster and steering axis inclination.
Camber and steering axis inclination:
Camber
-0°18' +- 45'
(-0.30° +- 0.75°)
Right-left error
30' (0.5°) or less
Steering axis inclination
12°36' +- 45'
(12.60° +- 0.75°)

30' (0.5°) or less

NOTICE:
Inspect while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the camber and caster is 30' or less.
Remove the camber-caster-steering axis inclination gauge.
Install the center ornament.
If the caster and steering axis inclination are not within the specified values after the camber has been correctly adjusted, recheck the suspension parts for damage and/or wear.
7. ADJUST CAMBER
Loosen the nut of the front suspension No.2 camber adjust cam.
Turn the front suspension No.1 camber adjust cam to adjust the camber.
Camber:
-0°18' +- 30' (-0.30° +- 0.5°)
NOTICE:
Inspect while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the camber and caster is 30' or less.
Be sure to inspect toe-in after the camber has been adjusted.
HINT:
If the camber adjust cam is turned 1 notch to the left when viewing from the front of the vehicle (the bolt moves approximately 1 mm to the outside), the camber will change by approximately -0°13'.
Tighten the camber adjust cam nut.
Torque:
170 N*m{
1,730 kgf*cm
, 125 ft.*lbf
}
8. ADJUST CASTER
Loosen the lock nut of the strut bar (rear side).
Turn the adjust nut of the strut bar (front side) to adjust caster.

Before inspecting the wheel alignment, adjust the vehicle height to the specified value.

3. INSPECT TOE-IN
Bounce the vehicle up and down at the corners to stabilize the suspension and inspect the vehicle height.
Inspect the toe-in.
Toe-in:
A-B
Toe-in
(total)
2.0 +/- 2.0 mm (0.08 +/- 0.08 in.)
HINT:
If the toe-in is not within the specified range, adjust it at the rack ends.
4. ADJUST TOE-IN
Measure the thread lengths of the right and left rack ends.
Standard:
Difference in thread length of 3.0 mm (0.12 in.) or less
Remove the rack boot set clips.
Loosen the tie rod end lock nuts.
Adjust the rack ends if the difference in thread length between the right and left rack ends is not within the specified range.
Extend the longer rack end if the measured toe-in deviates toward the outer-side.
Shorten the shorter rack end if the measured toe-in deviates toward the inner-side.
Turn the right and left rack ends by an equal amount to adjust the toe-in.
HINT:
Try to adjust the toe-in to the center of the specified range.
Make sure that the lengths of the right and left rack ends are the same.
Standard:
2.0 +/- 1.0 mm (0.08 +/- 0.04 in.)
Tighten the tie rod end lock nuts.
Torque:
74 N*m{
755 kgf*cm
, 55 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Temporarily tighten the lock nut while holding the hexagonal part of the steering rack end so that the lock nut and the steering rack end do not turn together.
Hold the width across flat of the tie rod end and tighten the lock nut.
Place the boots on the seats and install the clips.
HINT:
Make sure that the boots are not twisted.
5. INSPECT WHEEL ANGLE
Turn the steering wheel fully left and right and measure the turning angle.
NOTICE:
Perform the inspection while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the camber and caster is 30' (0.5°) or less.
Wheel turning angle:
Inside Wheel
38°00' + 1°30', -2°30'
(38.00° + 1.5°, -2.5°)
Outside Wheel:
Reference
33°00' + 1°30', -2°30'
(33.00° + 1.5°, -2.5°)
If the right and left inside wheel angles differ from the specified range, check the right and left rack end lengths.
6. INSPECT CAMBER, CASTER AND STEERING AXIS INCLINATION
Put the front wheel on the center of the alignment tester.
Remove the center ornament.
Install the camber-caster-steering axis inclination gauge at the center of the axle hub or drive shaft.
Inspect the camber, caster and steering axis inclination.
NOTICE:
Perform the inspection while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the camber and caster is 30' (0.5°) or less.
Camber and steering axis inclination:
Camber
-0°18' +/- 45'
(-0.30° +/- 0.75°)
Steering axis inclination
12°36' +/- 45'
(12.60° +/- 0.75°)

Remove the camber-caster-steering axis inclination gauge.
Install the center ornament.
If the caster and steering axis inclination are not within the specified values after the camber has been correctly adjusted, recheck the suspension parts for damage and/or wear.
7. ADJUST CAMBER
Loosen the nut on the front No. 2 suspension camber adjust cam.
Turn the front No. 1 suspension camber adjust cam to adjust the camber.
Camber:
-0°18' +/- 30' (-0.30° +/- 0.5°)
NOTICE:
Perform the inspection while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the camber and caster is 30' (0.5°) or less.
Be sure to inspect toe-in after the camber has been adjusted.
HINT:
If the camber adjust cam is turned 1 notch to the left when viewing from the front of the vehicle (the bolt moves approximately 1 mm to the outside), the camber will change by approximately -0°13' (-0.22°).
Tighten the camber adjust cam nut.
Torque:
170 N*m{
1730 kgf*cm
, 125 ft.*lbf
}
8. ADJUST CASTER
Loosen the lock nut on the strut bar (rear side).
Turn the adjust nut on the strut bar (front side) to adjust caster.
NOTICE:
Perform the inspection while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the caster is 30' (0.5°) or less.
Be sure to inspect toe-in after the caster has been adjusted.
HINT:
If the adjust nut of the strut bar (front side) is moved approximately 1 mm (0.04 in.), the caster will change by approximately 0°08' (0.13°).

Bounce the vehicle up and down at the corners to stabilize the suspension and inspect the vehicle height.
Measuring points:
A:

B:

Before inspecting the wheel alignment, adjust the vehicle height to the specified value.
The standard value shown here is a value that is used for adjusting the wheel alignment and does not indicate the height of an actual vehicle.

3. INSPECT TOE-IN
Bounce the vehicle up and down at the corners to stabilize the suspension and inspect the vehicle height.
Inspect the toe-in.
Toe-in:
A-B
Toe-in
(total)
2.0 +/-2.0 mm (0.08 +/-0.08 in.)
HINT:
If the toe-in is not within the specified range, adjust it at the rack ends.
4. ADJUST TOE-IN
Measure the thread lengths of the right and left rack ends.
Standard:
Difference in thread length of 3.0 mm (0.12 in.) or less
Remove the rack boot set clips.
Loosen the tie rod end lock nuts.
Adjust the rack ends if the difference in thread length between the right and left rack ends is not within the specified range.
Extend the longer rack end if the measured toe-in deviates toward the outer-side.
Shorten the shorter rack end if the measured toe-in deviates toward the inner-side.
Turn the right and left rack ends by an equal amount to adjust the toe-in.
HINT:
Try to adjust the toe-in to the center of the specified range.
Make sure that the lengths of the right and left rack ends are the same.
Standard:
2.0 +/-1.0 mm (0.08 +/-0.04 in.)
Tighten the tie rod end lock nuts.
Torque:
74 N*m{
755 kgf*cm
, 55 ft.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Temporarily tighten the lock nut while holding the hexagonal part of the steering rack end so that the lock nut and the steering rack end do not turn together.
Hold the width across flat of the tie rod end and tighten the lock nut.
Place the boots on the seats and install the clips.
HINT:
Make sure that the boots are not twisted.
5. INSPECT WHEEL ANGLE
Turn the steering wheel fully left and right and measure the turning angle.
NOTICE:
Perform the inspection while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the camber and caster is 30' (0.5°) or less.
Wheel turning angle:
Inside Wheel
38°00' +1°30', -2°30'
(38.00° +1.5°, -2.5°)
Outside Wheel:
Reference
33°00' +1°30', -2°30'
(33.00° +1.5°, -2.5°)
If the right and left inside wheel angles differ from the specified range, check the right and left rack end lengths.
6. INSPECT CAMBER, CASTER AND STEERING AXIS INCLINATION
Put the front wheel on the center of the alignment tester.
Remove the center ornament.
Install the camber-caster-steering axis inclination gauge at the center of the axle hub or drive shaft.
Inspect the camber, caster and steering axis inclination.
NOTICE:
Perform the inspection while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the camber and caster is 30' (0.5°) or less.
Camber inclination:

Remove the camber-caster-steering axis inclination gauge.
Install the center ornament.
If the caster and steering axis inclination are not within the specified values after the camber has been correctly adjusted, recheck the suspension parts for damage and/or wear.
7. ADJUST CAMBER
Loosen the nut on the front No. 2 suspension camber adjust cam.
Turn the front No. 1 suspension camber adjust cam to adjust the camber.
Camber inclination:
Except
TRH213R-KDPNKT
KDH212L-KDPNY
-0°20' +/-30'
(-0.3° +/-0.5°)
TRH213R-KDPNKT
KDH212L-KDPNY
-0°25' +/-30'
(-0.4° +/-0.5°)
NOTICE:
Perform the inspection while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the camber and caster is 30' (0.5°) or less.
Be sure to inspect toe-in after the camber has been adjusted.
HINT:
If the camber adjust cam is turned 1 notch to the left when viewing from the front of the vehicle (the bolt moves approximately 1 mm to the outside), the camber will change by approximately -0°13' (-0.22°).
Tighten the camber adjust cam nut.
Torque:
170 N*m{
1730 kgf*cm
, 125 ft.*lbf
}
8. ADJUST CASTER
Loosen the lock nut on the strut bar (rear side).
Turn the adjust nut on the strut bar (front side) to adjust caster.
NOTICE:
Perform the inspection while the vehicle is empty.
The maximum tolerance of right and left difference for the caster is 30' (0.5°) or less.
Be sure to inspect toe-in after the caster has been adjusted.
HINT:
If the adjust nut of the strut bar (front side) is moved approximately 1 mm (0.04 in.), the caster will change by approximately 0°08' (0.13°).

### FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER > DISPOSAL

FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER > DISPOSAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. DISPOSE OF FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER
With the piston rod extended, hold the front shock absorber obliquely in a vise.
Using a saw or cutting tools, slowly make a hole at approximately 20 mm (0.79 in.) from the weld in the lower end of the front shock absorber to discharge the gas inside.
NOTICE:
The gas is colorless, odorless and harmless.
Cover the saw or cutting tools with a shop rug or a piece of cloth to prevent shards of metal from flying about when discharging the gas.

---

### FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER > INSTALLATION

FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. TEMPORARILY TIGHTEN FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER
Install front shock absorber No.1 cushion retainer, front shock absorber No.2 cushion retainer, and the front shock absorber cushion to the front shock absorber.
NOTICE:
Install the cushion retainers in the correct direction.
Install the front shock absorber, front shock absorber No.1 cushion retainer , front shock absorber No.2 cushion retainer, and the front shock absorber cushion to the vehicle, and temporarily tighten a new lock nut.
NOTICE:
Install the cushion retainers in the correct direction.
Hold the width across flat of the piston rod and tighten the lock nut with an adjustable wrench until the protrusion length of the thread is the standard length.
Standard:
3 +- 1 mm (0.12 +- 0.04 in.)
Install the front shock absorber to the front suspension lower arm. Insert the bolt from the front side of the vehicle and temporarily tighten the bolt.
2. INSTALL FRONT WHEEL
Torque:
100 N*m{
1020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}
3. STABILIZE SUSPENSION
Bounce the vehicle up and down several times to stabilize suspension.
4. FULLY TIGHTEN FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER
Tighten the bolt on the lower side of the front shock absorber.
Torque:
105 N*m{
1,070 kgf*cm
, 77 ft.*lbf
}

---

### FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER > INSPECTION

FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER > INSPECTION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSPECT FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER
Compress and extend the shock absorber rod 4 or more times. Then check that there is no abnormal resistance or sound and operation resistance is normal.
If there is any abnormality, replace the front shock absorber with a new one.
NOTICE:
When disposing of the front shock absorber, see DISPOSAL (
Click here
).

---

### FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER > REMOVAL

FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER > REMOVAL
for Preparation
Click here
1. REMOVE FRONT WHEEL
2. REMOVE FRONT SHOCK ABSORBER
Hold the width across flat of the piston rod and remove the lock nut with an adjustable wrench.
Remove the front shock absorber No.1 cushion retainer, front shock absorber No.2 cushion retainer, and the front shock absorber cushion, and separate the front shock absorber from the bracket.
Remove front shock absorber No.1 cushion retainer, front shock absorber No.2 cushion retainer, and the front shock absorber cushion from the front shock absorber.
Remove the bolt and front shock absorber.

---

### FRONT UPPER SUSPENSION ARM > INSTALLATION

FRONT UPPER SUSPENSION ARM > INSTALLATION
for Preparation
Click here
1. INSTALL FRONT UPPER SUSPENSION ARM
Install the front suspension upper arm with the 3 bolts.
Torque:
190 N*m{
1,940 kgf*cm
, 140 ft.*lbf
}
2. INSTALL VANE PUMP OIL RESERVOIR ASSEMBLY
Install the vane pump oil reservoir assembly with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
8.0 N*m{
82 kgf*cm
, 71 in.*lbf
}
3. CONNECT FRONT UPPER SUSPENSION ARM
Connect the front suspension upper arm to the steering knuckle with the nut.
Torque:
113 N*m{
1,150 kgf*cm
, 83 ft.*lbf
}
Install a new cotter pin.
NOTICE:
If the holes for the cotter pin are not aligned, tighten the nut up to 60°.
Do not damage the ball joint dust cover.
4. CONNECT FRONT SPEED SENSOR
Connect the front speed sensor with the 2 bolts and 2 clips.
Torque:
8.0 N*m{
82 kgf*cm
, 71 in.*lbf
}
NOTICE:
Do not twist the sensor wire when installing.
Keep the sensor tip free from foreign matter.
5. INSTALL FRONT SUSPENSION TORQUE ARM
Install the front suspension torque arm to the suspension upper arm with the 2 bolts.
Torque:
81 N*m{
826 kgf*cm
, 60 ft.*lbf
}
6. TEMPORARILY TIGHTEN TORSION BAR SPRING
Check the right-left identification on the rear ends of the torsion bar springs.
NOTICE:
The end with an identification mark must be installed to the rear of the vehicle.
Wrap the splines on both ends of the torsion bar spring with protective tape.
Install the torsion dust cover to the torsion bar spring.
NOTICE:
Do not damage the lip of the dust cover when installing.
Remove the protective tape from the spline on both ends of the torsion bar spring and apply MP grease No.2 to them.
Align the matchmarks on the torsion bar spring and torque arm and engage the splines (for anchor arm side).
Align the matchmarks on the torsion bar spring and anchor arm and engage the splines (for torque arm side).
Install the following parts to the anchor arm.
Anchor arm swivel. (Part A)
Anchor arm adjusting hexagon bolt. (Part B)
Anchor arm adjusting seat. (Part C)
Anchor arm adjust nut. (Part D)
Tighten the anchor arm adjust nut so that the protrusion length of the thread of the anchor arm adjusting hexagon bolt equals the length of dimension A in the table below.

Install the fender apron mudguard seal with the 4 clips.

Torque:

1,020 kgf*cm
, 74 ft.*lbf
}
9. STABILIZE SUSPENSION
10. FULLY TIGHTEN TORSION BAR SPRING

Torque:

(
Click here
